TL;DR: 提出Janus框架，通过多智能体模拟合成轨迹，结合前瞻预测与安全判定任务，训练前瞻性安全守卫Vanguard，在4个基准上平均保护率提升15.9%，良性任务完成率提升5.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体安全正从内容审核转向预防工具使用前的操作失败，现有方法缺乏对长时延风险的前瞻能力，需要能在部分轨迹中预判延迟风险的守卫机制。

Method: 通过多智能体模拟合成多样化轨迹，学习共享策略的两个耦合任务：前瞻任务预测安全相关未来，判定任务基于观察前缀和预测未来进行安全决策，两任务通过CoAA-RL联合优化，用前瞻对下游判定的效用作为奖励。

Result: 在四个智能体安全基准上，Vanguard平均保护率比基线提高15.9个百分点，同时良性任务完成率提升5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: Janus框架通过前瞻性预测和联合优化有效提升了智能体在长时延任务中的安全性，在阻止不安全操作的同时减少了误拦截。

Abstract: Agent safety is moving from content moderation toward preventing operational failures before tool-using agents act. We propose Janus, a foresight-oriented framework for long-horizon agent safety that trains guards to anticipate delayed risks from partial trajectories. Janus synthesizes diverse agent trajectories via multi-agent simulation and learns a shared policy with two coupled tasks: an anticipation task that forecasts safety-relevant futures and an adjudication task that decides safety from both the observed prefix and anticipated future. The two tasks are jointly optimized with CoAA-RL, which rewards forecasts by their utility for downstream safety judgment. The resulting guard model, Vanguard, blocks unsafe actions before execution. Across four agent-safety benchmarks, Vanguard improves average protection by 15.9 percentage points over baseline guards while increasing benign task completion by 5.1 percentage points.

</details>


### [107] [Long-Term Sequential Decision Making under Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19914)
*Irmaan,Mirzanejad,Nadjet Bourdache,Abdel-Illah Mouaddib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ERQDP方法，通过离散化回报网格上的精确动态规划求解基于秩依赖风险目标的有限视界MDP规划问题，无需枚举或采样，可提供显式上下界证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限视界MDP在根式（决断型）风险目标下的规划问题中，目标函数对回报分布呈非线性，破坏了Bellman最优性原理，导致直接通过场景树枚举优化在计算上不可行。

Method: ERQDP方法包含三个核心步骤：(1) 通过精确动态规划求解秩-分位数替代目标；(2) 在离散化回报网格上对回报概率质量函数进行精确DP评估候选策略，附带显式舍入界；(3) 在任意循环中优化替代目标，报告目标函数在离散化预算下的显式上下界间隙（证书）。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，ERQDP返回带证书的解或显式残差间隙，支持快速风险参数扫描并大幅提升运行效率，同时兼容风险厌恶和风险寻求行为。

Conclusion: ERQDP提供了一种免枚举、免采样的可行方案，能够在有限视界MDP下有效处理秩依赖风险目标，并给出带理论保证的近似解。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon MDP planning under \emph{root-based} (resolute) risk objectives that apply a rank-dependent functional to the distribution of total returns. Such objectives are non-linear in the return distribution and generally break Bellman optimality, so direct optimization by scenario-tree enumeration is intractable. We propose \textbf{ERQDP}, an enumeration-free and sampling-free method that solves a rank--quantile surrogate via exact DP (Dynamic Programming), evaluates candidate policies exactly by DP over return Probability Mass Functions (PMFs) on a discretized return grid (with an explicit rounding bound), and refines the surrogate in an anytime loop that reports an explicit upper--lower gap (certificate) for the target objective up to discretization budgets. Across tested benchmarks, ERQDP returns certified solutions or explicit residual gaps, enables fast risk-parameter sweeps with substantial runtime gains, and supports both risk-averse and risk-seeking behaviors.

</details>


### [108] [MOF-Sleuth: Tool-Grounded Reward Alignment for Explainable Fine-Grained MOF CIF Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19935)
*Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Diandian Guo,Kun Peng,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Chaozhuo Li,Zhiyuan Ma,Yanbing Liu,Guobin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOF-Sleuth是一个基于强化学习的MOF晶体信息文件（CIF）审计智能体，通过司法实验室模块提取化学证据，结合强化学习驱动的推理引擎，实现对MOF结构错误的细粒度诊断和可解释检测，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个问题：(i) 细粒度归因不足：MOF验证器和机器学习模型只能提供固定检查或粗粒度标签，缺乏基于证据的解释；(ii) 不可靠的CIF推理：直接使用LLM审计成本高且不可靠，因为化学证据隐含在原子位点记录中，需要几何、连接性、占据率和电荷计算。根本原因在于化学证据与语言模型解释之间耦合薄弱。

Method: 提出MOF-Sleuth，包含两个模块：(1) 确定性司法实验室（Forensic Lab），推导组成、几何、连接性、占据率、配位和电荷证据；(2) Sleuth推理引擎，利用这些证据生成基于证据的解释、错误类型和二元决策。通过奖励引导的强化学习（RL）将工具测量转化为化学解释层面的监督信号，同时奖励最终答案、引用的化学证据和证据支持的诊断。提出Chemically Grounded Diagnosis (Chem-GD)指标评估诊断是否正确且基于事实相关的CIF证据。

Result: 在四个基准测试上，MOF-Sleuth在基于LLM的方法和MOF专用机器学习方法中均达到SOTA性能，在检测、归因和基于证据的解释质量方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: MOF-Sleuth通过强化学习桥接化学证据与语言模型解释，有效解决了CIF审计中的细粒度归因和推理可靠性问题，为计算化学中的可解释诊断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large metal-organic framework (MOF) databases support simulation, screening, and machine learning through crystallographic information files (CIFs). Subtle chemical and structural errors in these inputs can compromise downstream results and hinder manual inspection. LLM advances in computational chemistry offer paths beyond predictive screening toward fine-grained diagnosis with evidence-grounded explanations. However, two challenges remain: (i) limited fine-grained attribution: MOF-specific validators and machine-learning models scale detection but provide fixed checks, readiness scores, or coarse labels rather than evidence-grounded explanations; and (ii) unreliable CIF reasoning: direct LLM auditing is costly and unreliable because chemical evidence is implicit across atom-site records and requires geometric, connectivity, occupancy, and charge calculations. Both stem from weak coupling between chemical evidence and language-model explanation. We introduce MOF-Sleuth, a reinforcement-guided CIF auditing agent with two modules: a deterministic Forensic Lab and a Sleuth reasoning engine. The Lab derives composition, geometry, connectivity, occupancy, coordination, and charge evidence, and Sleuth uses this evidence to produce an evidence-grounded explanation, error types, and a binary decision. Reward-guided reinforcement learning (RL) turns tool measurements into chemical explanation-level supervision, rewarding not only the final answer but also cited chemical evidence and evidence-supported diagnoses. We introduce Chemically Grounded Diagnosis (Chem-GD), a metric that assesses whether a correct diagnosis is explained by factual, relevant CIF-derived evidence. Across four benchmarks, MOF-Sleuth establishes state-of-the-art performance among LLM-based approaches and MOF-specific machine-learning methods, demonstrating gains in detection, attribution, and grounded explanation quality.

</details>


### [109] [SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19949)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoyang Li,Xiaoxuan Qiao,Zhilang Wei,Tianming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SenWorld是一个物理接地、确定性、事件驱动的数字孪生仿真系统，通过构建具有固定ground truth的上下文丰富评估数据来解决智能手机助手评估中的数据隐私和标签可靠性问题。在北京16个虚拟人物的实验中，生成数据在类别分布（JSD 0.070）和日常通信节奏（JSD<0.1）上与真实用户基准高度吻合，并成功暴露了生产级助手中的78个故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机个人助手需要对纵向个人数据进行推理，但评估需要已知正确答案的上下文丰富数据，而真实设备轨迹因隐私敏感性无法共享。现有方法依赖事后标注或LLM评判，标签可靠性存疑。

Method: SenWorld采用物理接地、确定性、事件驱动的数字孪生仿真方法：从真实地图、天气、节假日和网络数据构建世界；虚拟人物在其中度过完整一天；所有可观测信号存档为全系统快照；每个评估案例通过指向已有记录的指针进行标注，而非事后标注或LLM评判。

Result: 在北京16个虚拟人物实验中，生成数据在类别分布（JSD 0.070）和日常通信节奏（JSD<0.1）上与真实用户基准高度匹配；形成完全互惠对话子图和差异化行为库；投射为717个评估案例后，在生产级助手中发现78个故障，集中在通话和短信记录，确认均为助手端检索错误。

Conclusion: SenWorld提供了一条隐私安全、可重复、经分布校验的评估数据生成路径，其标签通过构造固定，无需LLM评判介入。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [110] [EvoThink: Evolving Thinking in Large Reasoning Models via Self-Pruning and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19962)
*Xinbang Dai,Zheyu Xin,Huikang Hu,Lin Ren,Rihui Jin,Guohui Xiao,Guilin Qi,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Yuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoThink是一个通过自剪枝训练（SPT）减少冗余推理步骤，并结合突现时刻偏好优化（AMPO）利用失败推理尝试来同时提升大推理模型（LRM）推理效率和能力的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LRM常因冗余验证步骤导致"过度思考"，而现有缓解方法（如快慢思考切换、推理轨迹压缩）无法细粒度区分有益和冗余步骤，可能在追求效率时损害推理能力。因此需要一种同时提升推理效率和能力的方法。

Method: EvoThink包含两个关键组件：1) 自剪枝训练（SPT）：无监督方法，迭代式剪枝冗余推理步骤并在精简轨迹上自我训练；2) 突现时刻偏好优化（AMPO）：受遗传算法启发，识别有价值的失败推理尝试，合成"从错到对"的突现时刻数据，优化模型内化该推理模式。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上的广泛评估表明，EvoThink不仅大幅减少了推理时的token使用量，还提升了LRM的推理能力。

Conclusion: EvoThink通过细粒度区分并剪枝冗余步骤、同时利用失败尝试进行学习，能够在不牺牲推理能力的前提下显著提升推理效率，实现效率和能力的双赢。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often suffer from overthinking due to redundant verification steps. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a framework that reduces redundant verification and encourages the exploration of new reasoning paths. EvoThink comprises two key components: Self-Pruning Training (SPT), an unsupervised method that iteratively prunes redundant reasoning steps and self-trains on the concise trajectories; and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO), which, inspired by genetic algorithms, identifies valuable failed reasoning attempts, synthesizes from-wrong-to-right aha-moment data, and optimizes the model to internalize this reasoning pattern. Extensive evaluations across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that EvoThink not only substantially reduces inference-time token usage but also improves the reasoning capability of LRMs.

</details>


### [111] [The Giant Hippocampus: From Structural Monoculture to a System of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19973)
*Jaeho Seol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI领域将Transformer作为通用模板扩展到所有任务的做法存在结构性错误。神经科学证据表明，不同认知功能依赖差异化的皮层结构（如视觉皮层的密集第4层、运动皮层的厚第5/6层），而非单一模板的缩放。CNN的成功恰恰源于嵌入了局部感受野和层级深度等结构先验，而Transformer在硬件偶然性下成为路径依赖而非原则选择。Mixture-of-Experts并未提供真正的多样性。本文认为Transformer本质上是海马体的功能类似物，而非通用皮层，将其应用于所有任务如同将整个皮层视为一个巨大的布罗卡区。最后提出异构拓扑网络——一个由独立模块组成的系统之系统，各模块保留各自计算所需的归纳偏置，通过标准化接口通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI领域存在一种错误假设：可以用单一架构（Transformer）通过缩放解决所有任务，这与神经科学中"不同认知功能由不同皮层结构实现"的实证证据严重矛盾。本文旨在揭示这一结构性错误是可测量的，并主张在架构设计中纳入结构先验。

Method: 论文采用功能主义分析方法，从细胞构筑学（从Brodmann到Patch-seq）的百年证据出发，对比CNN的成功案例与Transformer的局限性，论证Mixture-of-Experts并未提供真正的结构多样性，最终提出异构拓扑网络作为替代方案。

Result: 论证了Transformer是海马体的功能类似物而非通用皮层，将其应用于听觉、执行门控、工作记忆等任务属于功能错配；指出现有模型架构缺乏模块间的结构差异化。

Conclusion: 提出异构拓扑网络作为设计规范：在训练前指定模块化结构，利用结构证据作为设计输入而非从训练后模型行为中逆向工程架构，要求不同模块保留各自计算所需的归纳偏置并通过标准化接口通信。

Abstract: AI researchers describe state-of-the-art models as one thing repeated at scale: the Transformer, wired identically for text, pixels, or speech. Neuroscientists describe the cortex as a mosaic - dense Layer 4 in visual cortex for spatial encoding, thick Layers 5/6 in motion cortex for temporal integration - different jobs solved by different structures. This paper argues the gap is a structural error, not a stylistic one, and is measurable. A century of cytoarchitecture, from Brodmann to single-cell Patch-seq, shows distinct cognitive functions are implemented by qualitatively different structures, not by rescaling one template. The convolutional neural network is the field's own proof: local receptive fields and hierarchical depth encoded this prior directly, reaching strong image recognition on far less data than later architectures needed. The paper traces how this lesson was discarded: the "Hardware Lottery" made the Transformer the path of least resistance, not the principled choice, and Mixture-of-Experts, often cited as diversity, in fact partitions parameters among identical experts. A functionalist analysis shows the Transformer is best understood as a functional analog of the hippocampal formation, not a general-purpose cortex - the same mistake as treating cortex as one giant Broca's area, except the field has now standardized on a giant hippocampus, applied to tasks it was never built for: audition, executive gating, working memory. The paper closes with an alternative: a Heterogeneous Topological Network, a System of Systems in which distinct modules keep the inductive bias their computation demands and communicate through standardized interfaces. This is a design discipline for AI architects, not cognitive science: specify modularity before training, using structural evidence as a design input rather than reverse-engineering architecture from a trained model's behavior.

</details>


### [112] [Coordinating from Memory: Graph-Structured Experience Reuse for Multi-Agent Adaptation in Dynamic Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19985)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhanhui Lin,Zhaokun Yan,Youyang Ni,Chenjun Lei,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图结构经验记忆框架（GSEM），通过图神经网络检索历史协调经验来加速动态制造环境中多智能体系统对新扰动的适应，在动态柔性作业车间调度基准上相比最强基线降低了4.1%-10.0%的完工时间和33%-38%的适应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法在应对动态制造环境中的频繁扰动时，每次都将扰动视为独立事件，丢弃了宝贵的协调经验，导致无法加速未来适应过程。

Method: 提出GSEM框架，将历史协调经验编码为异质关系图（捕获任务依赖、机器状态和智能体协作模式），当新扰动发生时，利用GNN检索机制识别结构相似的历史片段，实现基于经验的策略适应。

Result: 在三种扰动类型的动态柔性作业车间调度基准上，GSEM相比最强记忆增强基线降低了4.1%-10.0%的完工时间和33%-38%的适应时间，且优势在高扰动频率下更明显。

Conclusion: 图结构编码和基于相似性的检索机制是必要的，学习到的协调模式具有良好的跨扰动泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamic manufacturing environments require multi-agent systems to coordinate effectively under frequent operational disturbances such as machine failures, urgent job arrivals, and processing time variations. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches treat each disturbance episode independently, discarding valuable coordination experience that could accelerate future adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Graph-Structured Experiential Memory (GSEM) framework for multi-agent coordination in dynamic manufacturing. The framework encodes historical coordination episodes as heterogeneous relational graphs that capture task dependencies, machine states, and inter-agent collaboration patterns. When a new disturbance occurs, a graph neural network-based retrieval mechanism identifies structurally similar past episodes, enabling experience-guided policy adaptation rather than learning from scratch. Experiments on dynamic flexible job-shop scheduling benchmarks with three disturbance types show that GSEM reduces makespan by 4.1%-10.0% and adaptation time by 33%-38% compared to the strongest memory-augmented baseline, with the advantage increasing under higher disturbance frequency. Ablation studies and cross-disturbance transfer experiments further validate the necessity of graph-structured encoding and similarity-based retrieval and demonstrate the cross-disturbance generalizability of learned coordination patterns.

</details>


### [113] [CLARK: Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19996)
*Yousef Khan,Luca Gherardini,Marco Maratea,Joel Arrais,Jose Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLARK是一个将知识图谱、符号规则挖掘和概率推理（基于LP$^{\text{MLN}}$形式化）整合在一起的框架，用于构建自适应、可解释的分类模型，在医学数据集上展现出更好的分类性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习模型在数据分布变化时性能下降，且难以整合先验知识，无法有效处理不确定性和动态变化的信息。

Method: 从CACTUS知识图谱出发，将图结构翻译为LP$^{\text{MLN}}$程序，通过符号学习器迭代挖掘候选规则，并利用概率权重学习对规则进行校准，实现对不确定性的推理和图结构的优化。

Result: 在两个医学数据集上的实验表明，CLARK提升了分类性能，并且推理具有更好的泛化性。

Conclusion: CLARK为构建自适应、可解释、知识驱动的分类模型提供了一种原则性方法。

Abstract: Machine Learning models are widely used for automating classification tasks by extracting statistical patterns from data. However, their performance deteriorates if the data distribution changes, making them ill-suited to handle uncertain and evolving information. Moreover, they provide limited support for integrating prior knowledge. To address these limitations, we present CLARK (Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs), a framework that integrates knowledge graphs, symbolic rule mining, and probabilistic reasoning under the Logic Programs with Markov Logic Networks (LP$^{\text{MLN}}$) formalism. Starting from CACTUS-derived KGs, CLARK translates graph structure into an LP$^{\text{MLN}}$ program and iteratively enriches it with candidate rules proposed by symbolic learners. These rules are calibrated through probabilistic weight learning, enabling reasoning under uncertainty and refinement of the underlying graph structure. We evaluate CLARK on two medical datasets, analysing both rule quality and downstream classification performance. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

</details>


### [114] [Safe Remediation as Risk-Constrained Intervention Decision in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20005)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Chenjun Lei,Qiao Li,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个风险约束的自动修复框架，将安全 remediation 建模为带约束的马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），通过三维风险分解（爆炸半径、可逆性、认知不确定性）和上下文自适应的人工参与门控机制，在降低39%的误修复率的同时提升2.5个百分点的修复成功率，并减少17%的告警升级负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化修复系统旨在生成操作而非判断是否应该干预，错误的修复成本往往高于不采取任何行动，而安全性仅靠人工审批事后保证，缺乏系统性的安全约束机制。

Method: 将安全修复建模为风险约束的干预决策问题，形式化为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），最大化修复成功率同时限制误修复率（FRR）；提出三维风险分解（爆炸半径、可逆性、认知不确定性）提供可解释的安全接口；设计上下文自适应的人机协同门控机制，根据值班负载和业务重要性动态调整升级策略；从历史事件日志离线学习完整策略。

Result: 在 Train Ticket 微服务基准（Chaos Mesh 故障注入+RCAEval 故障分类）上，FRR降低39%，修复成功率提升2.5个百分点，告警升级负载相对固定阈值变体减少17%。

Conclusion: 将安全约束显式建模为优化目标而非事后手段，能够在不牺牲修复成功率的前提下显著降低错误干预风险，并有效减轻运维人员负担。

Abstract: In modern IT operations (IT-Ops), the cost of an incorrect repair often exceeds the cost of no action at all. Yet existing automated remediation systems are designed to generate actions rather than to decide whether intervention is warranted, leaving safety as an afterthought enforced by manual approval. This paper makes three contributions to close this gap: (i) we reformulate safe remediation as a risk-constrained intervention decision problem and cast it as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which the agent maximizes repair success subject to a bounded false remediation rate (FRR); (ii) we introduce a three-dimensional risk decomposition comprising blast radius, reversibility, and epistemic uncertainty, providing operators with an interpretable per-action safety interface; and (iii) we design a context-adaptive human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate that turns escalation from a binary failsafe into a bandwidth-aware control layer responsive to on-call load and business criticality. The full policy is learned offline from historical incident logs, enabling explicit control of the expected FRR. Experiments on the Train Ticket microservice benchmark with Chaos Mesh fault injection and an RCAEval-aligned fault taxonomy show that our framework reduces FRR by 39% while improving repair success by 2.5 points over a strong runbook baseline, and reduces on-call escalation load by 17% relative to a fixed-threshold variant.

</details>


### [115] [EvoDRC: A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Automated DRC Violation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20019)
*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDRC是一个基于技能演化的智能体框架，用于自动化先进节点芯片设计中的DRC修复。它通过从无关参考设计蒸馏初始化逐层修复技能，并利用目标设计的可追溯修复经验持续演化这些技能。实验显示在DAC26 DRC基准测试的7个模块级设计上，DRV整体减少73.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进节点物理设计中DRC收敛仍然是主要瓶颈。尽管详细布线器具有规则感知能力，残留的DRV通常需要手动工程变更单迭代。自动化此过程具有挑战性，因为修复必须考虑复杂的几何交互、保持电路连通性并避免引入新的违例。

Method: EvoDRC框架包含：1) 从无关参考设计蒸馏知识初始化逐层修复技能；2) 将版图分解为有界修复区域并为每个区域分配LLM修复代理；3) 局部DRC分析、连通性检查和影响预览工具提供反馈；4) 将修复操作及其导致的DRV变化存入知识数据库，用于演化修复技能。

Result: 在DAC26 DRC基准测试的7个模块级设计上，EvoDRC相比报告基线实现了73.5%的总体DRV减少。

Conclusion: EvoDRC通过技能演化框架有效自动化了模块级DRC修复过程，显著减少了设计规则违例，展示了将大语言模型与可追溯修复经验相结合在物理设计自动化中的潜力。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [116] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对差分约束的全局传播器，利用最短路径算法实现边界一致性传播，并能在惰性子句生成求解器中生成解释，实验表明该方法显著优于传统的独立传播方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的有限域传播器将每个差分约束视为独立的传播器，虽然保证完备性但效率低下。已有高效算法（如最短路径）可用于处理差分约束的满足性与蕴含问题，但传播算法并未充分利用这些算法，导致求解速度不必要地缓慢。

Method: 构建一个全局的边界一致性传播器，同时处理所有差分约束；借鉴SAT模理论（SMT）中差分约束理论求解器的思想，但针对传播器需求进行适配；核心创新在于展示了如何为全局差分约束传播器生成传播解释，从而将其集成到惰性子句生成（LCG）求解器中。

Result: 实验表明，全局处理差分约束相比标准传播方法能显著提升求解性能。

Conclusion: 将差分约束全局化处理并集成到LCG求解器中是可行且高效的，未来可进一步推广到更复杂的约束系统。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [117] [Reading and Steering Representations of Materials-Science Mechanisms in an Open-Weight Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20058)
*Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过多种实验方法（词汇读取、状态几何分析、反事实基准测试、因果干预）系统性地检验了开源大语言模型google/gemma-4-E4B-it中材料科学机制信息的表征形式，发现物理关系在受控状态变换中比在绝对隐藏状态中更可观测，且模型内部表征对工程答案具有因果控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能够正确回答科学问题，但仅凭输出正确性无法判断模型是否真正理解或使用了背后的物理原理。本文旨在区分模型中物理知识的三种表征形式（概念可读性、本构定向性、因果可控性），以揭示模型对物理机制的真实表示程度。

Method: 结合匹配的直接词汇读取与Jacobian词汇读取、无选项状态几何分析、60条物理定律的反事实基准测试以及因果干预实验。使用50条保留的材料描述进行独立双向Jacobian镜头拟合，以及72个提示的基准测试进行机制特定的隐藏状态邻域分析，并通过精确图审计排除数值比较的混淆效应。

Result: 1) 独立拟合的Jacobian镜头在50条保留描述中重现了概念排名；2) 无目标词集实现了10个机制家族中9个的盲识别；3) 机制特定的隐藏状态邻域存在但图审计表明数值比较即可解释；4) 状态变换在60条冻结关系中正确排序了40条定向定律中的39条，而词汇控制接近随机水平；5) 双向干预在所有12个匹配案例中改变答案概率方向，反事实状态补丁跨机制和答案格式传递相反的决策信号。

Conclusion: 材料科学物理关系在模型受控状态变换中比在绝对隐藏状态中更为显著可观测，模型内部表征对工程答案具有因果控制作用，但表观物理组织可能部分来源于数值比较而非真正的物理理解。

Abstract: Large language models can answer scientific questions, yet a correct output does not reveal whether the model represents or uses the governing physics. Here we show that materials science mechanism information in the open-weight google/gemma-4-E4B-it model has three experimentally separable forms: concepts are readable in individual hidden states, constitutive orientation is carried by controlled transformations between states, and selected internal representations causally control engineering answers. We combine matched direct and Jacobian vocabulary readouts, option-free state geometry, a 60-law counterfactual benchmark and causal interventions. In 50 held-out materials descriptions, three independently fitted Jacobian lenses reproduced concept ranks, and target-free word sets from both readouts enabled blinded identification of 9 of 10 mechanism families. A separate 72-prompt benchmark produced mechanism-specific hidden-state neighborhoods, but an exact graph audit showed that this apparent physical organization was equally explained by numerical comparison. We therefore compared otherwise identical prompts in which only the direction of the physical input was reversed, asking whether the resulting hidden-state movement followed the supplied constitutive law. These state transformations ordered direct, physically neutral and inverse laws across 60 frozen relations and correctly oriented 39 of 40 directional laws, whereas lexical controls were near chance. Bidirectional interventions shifted answer probabilities toward or away from the physically appropriate outcome across all 12 matched cases, while counterfactual state patches transferred opposing decision signals across mechanisms and answer formats. Physical relationships were therefore more visible in controlled state changes than in absolute states alone.

</details>


### [118] [PRO-LONG: Programmatic Memory Enables Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20064)
*Alexis Fox,Junlin Wang,Paul Rosu,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRO-LONG是一个基于程序化记忆的轻量级上下文管理框架，通过维护完整的结构化交互日志并利用编码代理高效搜索历史，解决了长时域任务中信息保存与检索的权衡问题。在ARC-AGI-3基准上，PRO-LONG使基础编码代理平均提升18.0个百分点，匹配或超越SOTA专用框架（最高76.1% pass@1），同时节省4.2-5.8x token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域任务对LLM代理的感知、推理和探索能力构成持续挑战，现有上下文管理方法在信息保存完整性与检索效率之间存在显著权衡困境。

Method: PRO-LONG构建程序化记忆系统，维护完整的结构化交互日志，利用编码代理的搜索能力高效检索历史信息，实现全面的上下文管理与低成本精准检索。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3全公开集上，PRO-LONG使基础编码代理平均提升18.0个百分点；匹配或超越SOTA专用框架（最高76.1% pass@1）；token消耗减少4.2-5.8倍。在Fable 5上以$1,750总成本达到97.4% best@2。

Conclusion: 程序化记忆能够有效解决长时域任务中信息保存与检索的权衡问题，实现更高效、更低成本的LLM代理上下文管理。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require sustained perception, reasoning, and exploration, and are a persistent challenge for large language model (LLM) agents. This gap is reflected in their limited performance on continual learning benchmarks such as ARC-AGI-3, especially when models are evaluated out of the box. Various agent harnesses have been proposed to close this gap, and each commits to a strategy for handling long sequences of observations, i.e., what information to save from the environment and how to load it into model context, a choice we argue is particularly consequential. Existing methods for context management face a significant tradeoff, as preserving more information makes retrieving relevant details less tractable. We propose PRO-LONG, a minimal context management framework built around programmatic memory for LLM agents in long-horizon, exploratory settings. PRO-LONG addresses the tradeoff by keeping a complete, structured interaction log and capitalizing on recent progress in coding agents to search this history efficiently. On the full ARC-AGI-3 public game set, PRO-LONG improves over a base coding agent by an average of 18.0 percentage points across frontier models, and matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized harnesses (up to 76.1% pass@1) while using 4.2-5.8x fewer tokens. With Fable 5, PRO-LONG achieves 97.4% best@2 at a total cost of \$1,750. Relevant code and logs are available at https://github.com/alexisfox7/PRO-LONG.

</details>


### [119] [TRUST-ESD: A Risk-Calibrated and Governance-Aware AI Framework for Enterprise Strategic Decision Support Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20065)
*Tian Qiu,Li Yan,Mahabubur Rahman Miraj,Shanqin Yi,Md Intekhab Rahman Galib,Jahid Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRUST-ESD框架，通过不确定性校准、风险评分、可解释性和治理合规等模块，为企业战略决策提供风险校准且治理感知的决策支持，相比基线方法在风险调整效用、风险暴露、CVaR、校准误差、解释保真度和治理合规性等指标上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略决策支持需要AI系统不仅准确，还需具备不确定性感知、风险校准、可解释性和治理合规性，现有预测驱动方法仅最大化期望效用，缺乏对风险与合规的综合考量。

Method: TRUST-ESD框架包含预测效用估计、共形不确定性校准、基于CVaR的下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、策略即代码治理、可解释性和人工监督等模块，通过多维度融合实现价值、可靠性、风险暴露与合规性的平衡推荐。

Result: 相比强不确定性感知基线，TRUST-ESD在风险调整效用提升7.95%、风险暴露降低23.22%、CVaR降低23.78%、校准误差降低13.89%、解释保真度提升10.90%、治理合规性提升9.76%，同时保持竞争性预测精度。消融与案例研究验证了各模块的联合贡献。

Conclusion: 不确定性校准、下行风险评分、风险记忆、可解释性和治理验证共同提升了可信赖的企业决策质量，TRUST-ESD为风险敏感且合规要求高的企业场景提供了有效的决策支持范式。

Abstract: Enterprise strategic decision support requires AI systems that are not only accurate, but also uncertainty-aware, risk-calibrated, explainable, and governance-compliant. This paper proposes TRUST-ESD, a risk-calibrated and governance-aware framework for enterprise decision support under uncertainty. TRUST-ESD evaluates feasible counterfactual strategies through predictive utility estimation, conformal uncertainty calibration, CVaR-based downside-risk scoring, risk-memory retrieval, policy-as-code governance, explainability, and human oversight. Unlike prediction-only methods that select actions by maximum expected utility, TRUST-ESD recommends strategies that balance value, reliability, risk exposure, and compliance. Experimental results show that TRUST-ESD improves risk-adjusted utility by 7.95%, reduces risk exposure by 23.22%, reduces CVaR by 23.78%, lowers calibration error by 13.89%, improves explanation fidelity by 10.90%, and increases governance compliance by 9.76% compared with strong uncertainty-aware baselines, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy. Ablation and case-study analyses further confirm that uncertainty calibration, downside-risk scoring, risk memory, explainability, and governance validation jointly improve trustworthy enterprise decision-making.

</details>


### [120] [CUSUM-Shaped Inference-Time Monitoring and Targeted Re-Decoding for Quantized Small Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20129)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MGT-B，一种基于监控引导的测试时回溯机制，通过检测推理轨迹中的不确定性和退化特征，在必要时回滚并重新解码，以改进量化小规模自回归推理模型的输出质量。在MATH-500数据集上，该方法在控制实验中准确率提升2.5个百分点（McNemar p=0.2632），在探索性分析中提升4.5个百分点（p=0.000753）。研究强调该结果仅支持选择性监控修复机制，而非通用或理论保证的推理改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化后的小型自回归推理模型在推理过程中常常陷入冗长、重复或无效的轨迹，而现有的推理时计算资源分配机制并未观测轨迹的发展状态。作者希望设计一种能够监控推理过程、检测退化信号并在必要时回溯修复的机制，从而在不改变模型本身的情况下提升推理质量。

Method: 基于改进的token级e-CUSUM控制器，MGT-B方法将重叠窗口中的采样前不确定性和退化特征映射为位置条件经验尾概率，通过CUSUM形式的重置累积混合投注因子。检测到警报后，估计回滚点，恢复token和KV缓存状态，并执行约束性重新解码。为减少选择偏差，作者构建了两种评估集：240对的时间顺序审核集（排除预阈值ID）和467对的探索性历史覆盖集。

Result: 在240对审核集上，准确率从82/240提升至88/240（+2.50个百分点，13次修正、7次回归，McNemar p=0.2632，配对bootstrap 95% CI [-1.25, +6.25]）。在467对历史覆盖集上，准确率从146/467提升至167/467（+4.50个百分点，p=0.000753），但该集包含200个阈值选择前/期间可用的种子-1 ID。所有316个无警报输出与原始输出完全一致，151个警报轨迹中包含29次修正和8次回归。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，MGT-B在MATH-500设定下支持一种选择性监控修复机制，而非通用或理论保证的推理改进。两种分析均非确认性分析，经验因子也未被确立为有效的e-process或e-detector。该方法对于解决量化小模型推理退化问题具有实用价值，但其效果有待在更广泛场景中验证。

Abstract: Quantized small autoregressive reasoning models can enter long, repetitive, or unproductive trajectories, yet inference-time compute is usually allocated without observing how a trajectory develops. Building on an earlier token-level e-CUSUM controller, we develop MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking), a revised external controller that maps overlapping windows of pre-sampling uncertainty and degeneration features to position-conditional empirical tail probabilities, accumulates mixture betting factors with a CUSUM-shaped reset, and responds to an alarm by estimating a rollback point, restoring token and key-value-cache state, and performing constrained re-decoding. To audit whether the effect persists on problem identities first observed after the manual choice of log threshold h = 10, we retrospectively exclude 260 IDs present in pre-threshold artifacts and retain the chronologically first post-threshold pair for each remaining ID, yielding a 240-pair chronology-audit set. On this set, accuracy changes from 82/240 to 88/240 (+2.50 percentage points; 13 corrections, 7 regressions; exact McNemar p = 0.2632; paired bootstrap 95% interval [-1.25, +6.25]). A broader 467-pair historical-coverage set of seed-matched pairs changes accuracy from 146/467 to 167/467 (+4.50 points; McNemar p = 0.000753), but includes 200 seed-1 IDs available before or during threshold selection and is reported only as an exploratory estimate. All 316 no-alarm outputs in the 467-pair set are identical to vanilla, while the 151 alarmed trajectories contain 29 corrections and 8 regressions. Neither analysis is confirmatory, and the empirical factors are not established as a valid e-process or e-detector. The results support a selective monitoring-and-repair mechanism for the studied MATH-500 setting, rather than a general or theoretically certified reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [121] [PoTRE: Test-Time Reasoning inspired by Cognitive Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20268)
*Anmol Kankariya,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoTRE是一种多智能体异构推理框架，通过解耦为对抗性精炼、分层策略规划、频谱搜索和直接链式四种智能体，并利用任务自适应聚合层融合多视角，在ARC-AGI-2、HLE和PRBench Finance基准上达到SOTA性能，其中HLE准确率达49.92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要长程规划和迭代纠错的复杂推理任务中表现不佳，且标准单流提示在面对新抽象概念或严格领域约束时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出PoTRE（多拓扑推理集成）异构框架，将推理解耦为四个智能体：对抗性精炼智能体、分层战略规划智能体、频谱搜索智能体和直接链式智能体，并通过任务自适应聚合层进行最终候选选择、语义合成或神经符号验证来产生全局鲁棒解。

Result: 在HLE基准上达到49.92%的SOTA准确率，超过此前最佳官方成绩；在ARC-AGI-2和PRBench Finance上也取得领先性能；与大规模同构基线相比，使用相似或更少的推理token实现了更优的推理性能。

Conclusion: 异构多智能体架构能在不增加推理计算成本的前提下显著提升LLM的复杂推理能力，为突破当前LLM推理瓶颈提供了有效路径。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at many tasks, they frequently struggle with complex reasoning that requires long-horizon planning and iterative error correction. Furthermore, standard single-stream prompting proves brittle when models encounter novel abstractions or rigorous domain constraints. We introduce PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles), a heterogeneous framework that decouples inference into four agents: (1) Adversarial Refinement Agent, (2) Hierarchical strategic Planning Agent, (3) Spectrum Search Agent, and (4) Direct Chain Agent. A final Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer dynamically reconciles these perspectives -- via final candidate selection, semantic synthesis, or neuro-symbolic verification -- to produce a robust global solution. We evaluate PoTRE on three frontier benchmarks: ARC-AGI-2, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and PRBench Finance. PoTRE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 49.92% on HLE, surpassing the previous best official score. We demonstrate that this architectural heterogeneity achieves improved reasoning performance using similar or fewer inference tokens compared to heavily scaled homogeneous baselines.

</details>


### [122] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出自然语言自编码器通过重建得分评估解释的忠实性存在结构性缺陷：重建得分仅反映整体大意而非具体事实，大量虚假声明不会被惩罚。实验表明标准方案会产生协同发展的私有编码（虚假措辞），即使修复也不起作用。作者提出两种审计协议和RECAP方法（在目标模型旁训练线性头以保持指定内容可解码），实验证明RECAP能消除私有编码、使解释包含真实内容，并抵抗对抗性攻击（AUC 0.95 vs 控制组0.51）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言自编码器通过重建得分评估解释忠实性，但该方法对单个虚假声明结构不敏感：翻转一个声明若不改变重建结果，该声明不会被惩罚。这种缺陷可能导致高重建得分但解释不忠实的问题。

Method: 1) 提出两种审计协议：grounded-vs-true交叉验证和评估器交换；2) 提出RECAP方法：与目标模型共同训练的线性头，用于保持指定内部内容的可解码性；3) 在Qwen-2.5-7B和Pythia-160M上进行实验验证。

Result: 1) 标准方案中约2%的特定声明是重建依赖的，得分追踪大意而非具体事实；2) 合成数据下5/5运行出现协同私有编码；3) RECAP使指定内容可探测解码，私有编码消失，代价仅+0.001 nat；4) RECAP使独立探针能区分真假声明（AUC 0.96 vs 0.82）；5) 对抗攻击下RECAP探针仍有效（AUC 0.95），控制组降至0.51。

Conclusion: 高重建得分不能作为单个声明忠实性的证据。RECAP使指定内部内容可独立验证，比仅靠模型生成的文字解释更可靠，对可解释性和AI安全均有重要意义。

Abstract: Natural-language autoencoders score explanations of hidden activations by reconstruction: an explanation is deemed faithful if the activation can be regenerated from it. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconstruction-dependent, so the score tracks gist, not specific facts. Under exact synthetic ground truth, the standard recipe develops co-adapted private codes (false wording the reconstruction depends on) in 5/5 runs, and fixes that leave the target model unchanged do not help. We contribute two audit protocols, the grounded-vs-true cross and the evaluator swap, and RECAP (Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors): linear heads trained alongside the target model to keep designated content decodable. On RECAP-trained sandbox models, fresh verbalizers state the designated content truly and the codes vanish, at a +0.001-nat cost. This replicates on a pretrained Pythia-160M: the content becomes reliably probe-decodable, though a fresh verbalizer conveys it only in part (truth 0.44-0.46 vs a near-zero control). For interpretability, high reconstruction does not certify individual claims. For AI safety, RECAP makes designated internal content independently checkable against probes rather than asserted by prose a model can game: an independent probe scores the verbalizer's true claims above its false ones (AUC 0.96, vs 0.82 without RECAP). Against an adversary that edits an explanation to maximize the reconstruction score while lying (suppressing ~87% of its lie penalty), the RECAP probe still flags the lies (AUC 0.95) while the control probe collapses to chance (0.51).

</details>


### [123] [SoftReason: A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Deductive Reasoning Architecture over High-Dimensional Perceptual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20402)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoftReason提出一种神经软符号架构，通过可微分的软解释张量和学习的蕴含算子，实现从感知到知识图谱推理的端到端可微分演绎推理，并在KVQA任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经符号系统在感知与演绎之间存在离散接口（梯度断裂），且推理中的前提通常需要从高维输入中推断而非直接给出离散符号，亟需一种可微分的端到端推理框架。

Method: 1) 将演绎状态表示为候选常量与谓词上的局部软解释张量；2) 感知模块输出概率化基础事实，知识图谱三元组作为高置信度软证据；3) 核心创新是可微分的蕴含算子提升：利用谓词定义嵌入和潜在组合通道形成软体谓词混合，聚合所有可能见证者，通过单调概率OR更新解释。

Result: 在知识感知视觉问答（KVQA）任务上实现了端到端的感知基础、知识图谱证据注入和可微分演绎闭包计算。

Conclusion: SoftReason弥合了感知与演绎之间的梯度鸿沟，首次在同一可训练架构中统一了感知基础、知识注入和演绎推理，为神经符号系统提供了新的可微分范式。

Abstract: In many reasoning problems, the premises are not observed as discrete symbols, but must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs. Further, the predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence are supplied by a Knowledge Graph (KG), or rule definitions. Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction. We present a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture for differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates. SoftReason removes the gradient gap by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. Our core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR. We instantiate the framework on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), and demonstrates how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Native Multi-Dimensional Subquadratic Operators via Input Dependent Long Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19378)
*David R. Wessels,Farhad Ramezanghorbani,David W. Romero,Alireza Moradzadeh,Olivia Viessmann,Maksim Zhdanov,John St. John,Ken Janik,David M Knigge,Yucheng Tang,Erik J Bekkers,Saee Gopal Paliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyenaND是一种亚二次、全局、输入依赖的算子，可直接处理多维数据的原生几何结构，通过隐式参数化的全局输入依赖多维卷积核实现。其CUDA实现nSubQ融合了FFT-卷积路径，将O(L log L)复杂度转化为实际加速。在长程基因组学、计算机视觉、医学影像和PDE建模等任务中，纯HyenaND堆叠匹配强注意力基线精度，而HyenaND与注意力层混合配置则优于纯注意力方法和强循环混合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制的亚二次替代方案在处理多维数据时需要做出妥协：标准卷积缺乏全局感受野和输入依赖性，而循环模型需要将图像、体积和PDE等数据栅格化为违反其空间结构的1D扫描顺序。作者希望设计一种能直接作用于多维数据原生几何结构、同时保持亚二次计算复杂度和全局输入依赖性的算子。

Method: HyenaND通过隐式参数化的全局输入依赖多维卷积核，在数据的原生几何结构上直接执行卷积操作。实现上采用nSubQ CUDA实现，融合FFT-卷积路径来加速O(L log L)的缩放过程。

Result: 纯HyenaND堆叠在长程基因组学、计算机视觉、医学影像和PDE建模等任务中匹配了强注意力基线（如Transformer）的精度；混合配置（HyenaND与注意力层交错）在各项任务上均优于纯注意力和基于循环的强混合方法。

Conclusion: HyenaND证明可以不依赖注意力机制，直接在多维数据的原生几何结构上实现全局、输入依赖的亚二次算子，在实际任务中达到或超越注意力的性能，为构建高效的长程依赖模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Subquadratic alternatives to attention require compromises when applied to multi-dimensional data: standard convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data such as images, volumes, and partial differential equation (PDE) into an ad-hoc $1\rm D$ scan order that violates their spatial structure. We introduce \textit{HyenaND}, a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator that acts directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Our CUDA implementation, \texttt{nSubQ}, fuses the FFT-convolution path to turn HyenaND's $\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$ scaling into wall-clock speedups. Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations that interleave HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

</details>


### [125] [Bayesian Wind Tunnels for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19379)
*Siddhartha R Dalal,Vishal Misra,Abhay Parekh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer能否进行贝叶斯模型选择（即从数据中识别正确的假设类）。通过引入模型选择贝叶斯风洞，使用不动点自由对合等关系型结构，2.8M参数Transformer在多个任务上达到与贝叶斯最优高度一致。但当判别统计量需要算术运算时，模型选择在不透明符号上完全失败，并且这一边界在112倍参数缩放后依然存在。头部子任务诊断将失败定位在头部反转与算术的组合上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明Transformer能在固定假设类内进行精确贝叶斯滤波，但能否进行贝叶斯模型选择（即识别正确的假设类）仍不清楚。本文旨在系统探究Transformer在这一更高层次推理任务上的能力及其局限性。

Method: 提出模型选择贝叶斯风洞作为受控环境，其中假设类上的真实后验可用闭式表达。使用不动点自由对合（f(f(x))=x）等纯关系型结构，以及不嵌套的比较（对合vs 3-循环），测试Transformer的模型选择能力。通过整数token和不透明符号（每轮含义改变）两种表征进行对比，并在不同参数量级（2.8M至316M）和不同算术运算（模加、模乘）条件下测试。

Result: 1）2.8M参数Transformer在纯关系型任务上达到0.01比特熵一致性（与贝叶斯最优）；2）不嵌套比较任务上类后验MAE低于0.001；3）当需要算术运算时，整数token成功但完全不透明符号完全失败，且该边界在112倍参数缩放下依然存在；4）固定重标定的不透明符号能成功（0.009-bit MAE），说明稳定语义而非整数身份是关键；5）头部子任务诊断将失败定位在头部反转与算术的组合；6）前沿LLM在相同任务上表现定性贝叶斯行为但校准差距大（约55倍）。

Conclusion: Transformer能够进行真正的贝叶斯模型选择（超越简单性/子集偏差），但在需要算术与不透明符号组合时存在根本性局限，这种局限无法通过单纯扩大模型规模克服。稳定语义（而非整数身份）是电路编译的关键条件。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that transformers can perform exact Bayesian filtering within a fixed
  hypothesis class. Can they also perform Bayesian model selection -- identifying the correct
  hypothesis class from data? We introduce model-selection Bayesian wind tunnels: controlled
  environments where ground-truth posteriors over hypothesis classes are available in closed
  form. Using fixed-point-free involutions -- whose defining property f(f(x))=x is purely
  relational -- a 2.8M-parameter transformer achieves 0.01-bit entropy agreement with the
  Bayesian optimum (3 seeds), with both integer tokens and opaque symbols whose meanings
  change every episode. This extends to non-nested comparisons: involutions vs. 3-cycles
  (where neither class is a subset of the other) achieve class-posterior MAE under 0.001,
  demonstrating genuine model selection beyond simplicity/subset bias. We then identify a
  sharp perceptual access condition: when the discriminative statistic requires arithmetic --
  modular addition (rotations) or multiplication (f(x)=cx mod p) -- model selection succeeds
  with integer tokens but fails completely with opaque symbols, and this boundary persists
  under 112x scaling (2.8M to 316M parameters). A stationarity control confirms the operative
  factor: opaque tokens with a fixed relabeling succeed (0.009-bit MAE), showing that stable
  semantics, not integer identity, enable circuit compilation. Header subtask diagnostics
  localize the failure to the composition of header inversion with arithmetic rather than
  header parsing itself. Probing frontier LLMs on the same tasks shows qualitative Bayesian
  behavior but a large calibration gap (~55x), measured through lossy probes and therefore
  directional rather than exact.

</details>


### [126] [CruiseBench: A Real-Flight-Aligned N-CMAPSS Benchmark for Engine RUL Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19380)
*Pu Cheng,Qiang Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CruiseBench，一个从N-CMAPSS数据集中提取的巡航阶段剩余寿命(RUL)基准测试，通过CPM-N-CMAPSS掩码工具识别巡航区间，并采用固定协议（排除虚拟传感器、健康参数等）实现标准化评估。实验表明TSMixer在RMSE和Saxena得分上表现最优，消融研究验证了飞行阶段选择、时间降采样方法和RUL上限阈值对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-CMAPSS数据集虽增加真实性（记录完整飞行阶段时间序列而非仅周期级快照），但全飞行记录数据量大、退化信号与运行状态变化相纠缠，导致预处理选择复杂、RUL建模性能难以直接比较。为解决这一可控评估难题，需要构建一个标准化的子基准测试。

Method: (1) 提出CPM-N-CMAPSS掩码工具，采用common-altitude方法识别各子数据集的巡航区间；(2) 构建CruiseBench固定协议：仅使用场景描述符和测量传感器作为输入，排除虚拟传感器、健康参数和辅助元数据，保留原始分辨率窗口，应用数据集级RUL上限；(3) 使用LSTM、GRU、TCN、TSMixer四种模型进行实验并提供基线结果；(4) 开展消融研究分析飞行阶段选择、时间降采样方法和RUL上限阈值的影响。

Result: 在CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10设置下，TSMixer获得最低平均RMSE（3.4±1.71）和最低Saxena得分（(2.50±2.99)×10^4）。消融实验表明飞行阶段选择、时间降采样方法和RUL-cap阈值对报告结果有显著影响。

Conclusion: CruiseBench通过固定巡航阶段协议为可控的RUL模型比较提供了可复现的子基准，CPM-N-CMAPSS为未来的迁移学习和领域自适应研究提供了阶段特定的数据基础。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction estimates how long an engine can continue safe operation and is central to maintenance planning. N-CMAPSS extends C-MAPSS by simulating run-to-failure aero-engine trajectories using recorded real-flight profiles and retaining complete within-flight time series rather than cycle-level snapshots. However, this added realism reduces evaluation control because full-flight records increase data volume and entangle degradation cues with operating-regime variation, complicating preprocessing choices and direct comparisons of RUL modeling performance. To mitigate this issue, this paper proposes CruiseBench, a cruise-stage RUL benchmark derived from N-CMAPSS. It introduces CPM-N-CMAPSS (Cruising-Period Mask for N-CMAPSS), a mask artifact that stores cycle-local cruising intervals identified by the common-altitude method for the nine accessible subdatasets. CruiseBench applies a fixed protocol to the masked rows, using scenario descriptors and measured sensors as inputs while excluding virtual sensors, health parameters, and auxiliary metadata from the feature tensor, preserving native-resolution windows, and applying dataset-wise RUL caps. Experiments with LSTM, GRU, TCN, and TSMixer provide baseline results for this setting. Under CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10, TSMixer obtains the lowest average RMSE, $3.4\pm1.71$, and Saxena score, $(2.50\pm2.99)\times 10^{4}$. Ablation studies show that flight-stage selection, temporal downscaling method, and RUL-cap threshold affect reported results. With its fixed cruise-stage protocol, CruiseBench provides a reproducible sub-benchmark for controlled RUL model comparison and CPM-N-CMAPSS provides a stage-specific data foundation for future transfer-learning and domain-adaptation studies.

</details>


### [127] [Air Quality Arena: A Large-Scale Multi-Region Ground Monitoring Dataset and Benchmark for Air Quality Forecasting with Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19381)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Manik Gupta,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Air Quality Arena (AQA)，一个涵盖7个国家、4大洲、6种主要污染物、超过14,000个站点-污染物序列的大规模空气质量基准数据集和评测平台，用于评估时间序列基础模型在短期空气质量预测上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空气质量预测基准在地理范围、污染物覆盖上都很狭窄，且未评估新一代时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在大规模真实数据上的表现。

Method: 构建了AQA-Data多国多污染物数据集（7国、4大洲、6种污染物、3年跨度、14,000+序列），并设计了AQA-Bench基准评测框架，对11个TSFMs和经典基线模型进行短期空气质量预测评估。

Result: TSFMs在零样本预测中有效且持续优于经典基线模型，最佳模型采用跨模态架构，利用视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。

Conclusion: AQA填补了大规模、多国家、多污染物空气质量基准的空白，证明了TSFMs在空气质量预测任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Air pollution causes an estimated 7.9 million premature deaths annually, making accurate forecasting a critical public health priority. Machine learning is increasingly being applied to forecast air pollution levels, yet existing benchmarks remain narrow in both geographic scope and pollutant coverage, and fail to evaluate the latest generation of time series foundation models (TSFMs) on real world, large scale data. We present Air Quality Arena (AQA), a large scale multi-country and multi-pollutant dataset (AQA-Data) and benchmark (AQA-Bench) to address this gap. AQA covers 6 major pollutants over a three year period across 7 diverse countries and 4 continents, with more than 14,000 station-pollutant series, aiming to provide a comprehensive benchmark for air quality tasks. We benchmark this dataset across 11 leading time series foundation models and classical baselines to assess performance on short-term air quality forecasting. Our results demonstrate that TSFMs are effective zero-shot forecasters and consistently outperform classical baselines, with our top-performing model employing a cross-modal architecture that leverages a vision foundation model for time series forecasting. AQA is publicly released at AirQualityArena.github.io

</details>


### [128] [Challenges of Explainability in Continual Learning for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19382)
*Quentin Besnard,Emmanuel Doumard,Nicolas Labroche,Nicolas Ragot,Nicolas Ringuet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索将可解释性作为理解基于经验回放的持续学习在非平稳时间序列预测中行为的核心工具，通过注意力展开和Grad-CAM等方法分析PatchMixer、PatchTST和DLinear等神经架构的预测行为与采样策略，揭示归因模式随时间演化的规律及其对数据选择和自适应策略的指导意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列预测中表现出潜力，但在实际环境监测部署中面临非平稳动态和可解释性不足的双重挑战，亟需理解持续学习框架下的模型行为。

Method: 采用PatchMixer、PatchTST、DLinear等神经预测架构，结合基于注意力机制的经验回放采样策略，利用注意力展开（attention rollout）和梯度类激活映射（Grad-CAM）等可解释性方法分析预测行为与采样策略。

Result: 在展现异质模式和状态转移的真实地下水位时间序列上，可解释性分析有效揭示了持续学习框架的动态行为，归因模式随时间演化，并能为非平稳场景下的数据选择和自适应策略提供指导。

Conclusion: 可解释性不仅是理解持续学习行为的有效工具，还能揭示归因模式的时变规律，为数据选择和模型自适应策略提供有价值的见解，同时指出了该方向面临的挑战与机遇。

Abstract: Deep learning models have shown strong potential for time series forecasting, yet their deployment in real-world environmental monitoring remains challenging due to non-stationary dynamics and limited explainability. In this work, we investigate explainability as a central tool for understanding continual learning in adaptive time series forecasting, with Experience Replay strategies. We study neural forecasting architectures such as PatchMixer, PatchTST and DLinear, augmented with attention-based sampling mechanisms to support model adaptation over time. Explainability is leveraged through attention rollout and gradient-based attribution methods (Grad-CAM) to analyze both predictive behavior and sampling strategies within a continual learning framework. Experiments conducted on real-world piezometric time series exhibiting heterogeneous patterns and regime shifts show that analyzing model and sampling behaviors provides valuable insights into the dynamics of the continual learning framework. Beyond predictive performance, our results highlight the challenges and opportunities of using explainability to understand continual learning behaviors, revealing how attribution patterns evolve over time and how they can inform data selection and adaptation strategies in non-stationary forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [129] [SUM: Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors for Federated Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19384)
*Jaeik Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦类增量学习（FCIL）中时空灾难性遗忘问题，提出SUM框架——一种纯服务器端的几何手术方法，通过对空间（客户端间）和时间（任务间）的适应向量进行几何调整，在不增加客户端计算/通信开销的情况下，显著提升FCIL性能（最高提升22%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FCIL方法通常用分离机制处理空间干扰（异构客户端）和时间干扰（顺序任务），导致额外的客户端计算或通信开销，且未能在聚合过程中调控更新之间的方向交互。作者希望设计一种纯服务器端、无需额外客户端开销的统一框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 将FCIL重新解释为统一的多任务学习问题，客户端和任务更新均表示为共享参数空间中的适应向量。提出SUM框架：（1）空间SUM：在每轮聚合中通过几何手术缓解客户端级干扰；（2）因果在线时间SUM：随时间推移消除跨任务干扰，无需额外客户端计算、通信或内存。

Result: 在多种视觉和语言基准上，SUM相比现有FCIL方法性能提升最高达22%，同时对不可靠客户端保持鲁棒性，并维持计算效率。

Conclusion: SUM通过纯服务器端的几何方法有效解决了FCIL中时空灾难性遗忘问题，在性能、鲁棒性和效率方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Real-world intelligent systems often require both distributed collaboration across data-isolated clients and continual adaptation to evolving tasks. This setting naturally gives rise to Federated Class Incremental Learning (FCIL), which combines Federated Learning (FL) and Continual Learning (CL). However, their combination introduces two coupled sources of interference: spatial interference from heterogeneous clients and temporal interference from sequential tasks, jointly leading to Spatial-Temporal Catastrophic Forgetting (ST-CF). Existing approaches typically address spatial and temporal interference with separate mechanisms, often incurring additional client-side computation or communication, while leaving directional interactions among updates during aggregation unregulated. In this paper, we reinterpret FCIL as a unified multi-task learning problem, where both client and task updates are represented as adaptation vectors in a shared parameter space. Based on this view, we propose Unified Geometric Surgery on Spatio-Temporal Adaptation Vectors (SUM), a purely server-side framework that performs geometric surgery on adaptation vectors during aggregation. Spatial SUM mitigates client-level interference within each round, while causal online temporal SUM removes cross-task interference over time without additional client-side computation, communication, or memory beyond standard federated training. Empirically, SUM achieves up to 22% improvement over prior FCIL methods across diverse vision and language benchmarks while remaining robust to unreliable clients and maintaining computational efficiency.

</details>


### [130] [STN-TGAT: Top-K Portfolio Construction via Prior-Guided Graph Attention with Learnable Soft-Threshold Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19385)
*Haoran Guo,Yutong Lu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STN-TGAT模型，融合时序Transformer与图注意力网络，结合NMI先验图与软阈值稀疏化机制，在考虑实际交易成本等条件下进行股票排名与投资组合构建，实证表明该方法在预测准确性和投资收益上均优于基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能同时有效建模时间动态性和横截面依赖性，且缺乏对实际投资条件（如交易成本、权重分配）的考虑，导致理论与实践的差距。

Method: 提出STN-TGAT模型：1) 时序Transformer捕获长期序列模式；2) 图注意力网络建模动态股票间关系；3) NMI先验图结合软阈值稀疏化机制去除噪声连接；4) 投资组合构建纳入Top-5选股、权重分配和交易成本调整。

Result: 在真实市场数据上，STN-TGAT在预测精度和投资组合收益方面均持续优于多个基准模型。

Conclusion: 将决策对齐训练与自适应关系建模相结合，为数据驱动的投资组合构建提供了一致且实际有效的框架。

Abstract: This paper tackles the problem of stock ranking and portfolio construction under realistic investment settings by jointly modeling temporal dynamics and cross-sectional dependencies. We propose the Soft-Threshold NMI-prior Transformer Graph Attention Network (STN-TGAT), which integrates a temporal Transformer with a Graph Attention Network to capture long-horizon sequential patterns and dynamic inter-stock relationships. An NMI-based prior graph combined with a soft-threshold sparsification mechanism enhances structural robustness by mitigating noisy correlations while preserving informative connections. The portfolio formation process incorporates practical considerations, including Top-5 selection within the Top-50 $S\&P$ 500 constituents, explicit weight allocation, and transaction cost adjustment, thereby aligning the evaluation with real-world trading conditions. Empirical results on real-world data demonstrate that STN-TGAT consistently outperforms benchmark models from predictive accuracy and investment profitability measured by portfolio returns. These findings suggest that combining decision-aligned training with adaptive relational modeling provides a coherent and practically effective framework for data-driven portfolio construction.

</details>


### [131] [Building Fast, Evaluating Slow: Pipeline Choices Dominate Autointerpretability Score Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19386)
*Sinie van der Ben,Neele Roch,Anna Hedström,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性实验发现，稀疏自编码器（SAE）的可解释性评分在不同评估流程下高度不稳定，方法变异超过架构变异，导致跨论文比较可能反映的是流程差异而非架构差异，并提出了稳定性检查工具和最低报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨论文比较SAE可解释性时常用自解释性评分，但评分是否反映特征的稳定属性而非评估流程的混杂因素尚未得到验证，作者旨在检验这一假设是否成立。

Method: 在四种指标（simulation, detection, fuzzing, purity）、两种模型（Pythia-160M, Apertus-8B）和四个方法变异轴上进行系统性实验，采用方差分解方法分析评分稳定性。

Result: 1) 方法变异总体上超过架构变异；2) 各指标稳定性不同，detection最稳定，fuzzing最不可靠；3) top-k特征排名在语料和采样条件间不一致，均值稳定掩盖了特征级的不稳定性，且不能通过监测解释相似性来检测。

Conclusion: 基于自解释性评分的跨论文比较可能反映流程差异而非架构差异，对SAE实用性的讨论有重要影响；不可靠的评估阻碍了可解释性研究进展，作者贡献了方差分解方法、稳定性检查和最低报告清单。

Abstract: Cross-paper comparison of sparse autoencoder (SAE) interpretability often relies on autointerpretability scores. In this evaluation pipeline, a language model (LM) explains each feature, and another LM scores the explanation. For these comparisons to be meaningful, scores must reflect stable properties of the features rather than confounding aspects of the evaluation pipeline. Through systematic experiments across four metrics (simulation, detection, fuzzing, purity), two models (Pythia-160M, Apertus-8B), and four axes of methodological variation, we show that this assumption does not hold. Specifically, we find that R1) methodological variance collectively exceeds architectural variance across all metrics and tested models; R2) each metric exhibits a distinct instability profile, with detection being the most stable and fuzzing unreliable across all conditions; R3) top-k feature rankings do not stay consistent across corpus and draw conditions, masking per-feature instability behind stable mean scores; a failure that cannot be detected by monitoring explanation similarity alone. These findings suggest that cross-paper comparisons based on autointerpretability scores may reflect pipeline differences rather than architectural differences, with implications for the ongoing debate on SAE utility. More broadly, unreliable evaluation slows progress in interpretability research at a time when reliable tools for understanding AI systems are needed. To support evaluation, we contribute a variance decomposition approach, a Stability Check, and a Minimum Reporting Checklist.

</details>


### [132] [Scale-Aware Learning of Chaotic Dynamics on Unstructured Meshes via Binned Spectral Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19387)
*Kanad Sen,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将频带谱损失函数扩展到非结构化网格上的代理模型方法，通过图拉普拉斯频率带替代傅里叶频带，并引入Chebyshev多项式滤波器和多级图结构（GLEAM）来高效提升混沌非线性动力系统长时间滚动的保真度和统计不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非线性混沌动力系统的代理建模需要保持物理场的尺度依赖结构，但现有基于傅里叶频带的谱损失函数仅适用于结构化网格，无法直接应用于非结构化网格，因为后者缺乏规范傅里叶基。

Method: 1) 将傅里叶频带替换为图拉普拉斯频带，实现非结构化网格上的频带谱功率匹配；2) 全谱形式利用图拉普拉斯特征空间，但计算成本高；3) 可扩展近似使用稀疏Chebyshev多项式图滤波器避免显式特征分解；4) 多级图架构中提出GLEAM（Graph Laplacian Energy Alignment for Meshes），在粗-细层级上施加保留子空间的尺度感知正则化。

Result: 在非结构化网格湍流预测任务中，所提谱损失函数相比确定性基线方法，提升了长时间滚动的保真度，并更好地保持了统计不变量。

Conclusion: 图拉普拉斯频带谱损失函数有效扩展了频带谱匹配思想至非结构化网格，Chebyshev近似和GLEAM多级对齐策略在保持计算可扩展性的同时显著改善了混沌动力系统代理模型的长期预测质量。

Abstract: Surrogate modeling for high-dimensional nonlinear dynamical systems that exhibit chaos requires mechanisms that preserve not only pointwise accuracy but also the scale-dependent structure of physical fields. Bandwise spectral power losses, such as the binned spectral loss function, provide such supervision on structured grids, where Fourier modes define a standard frequency decomposition. On irregular meshes, however, no canonical Fourier basis exists, and spectral representations must be constructed from graph operators induced by mesh connectivity and geometry. In this study, we extend the binned spectral power loss for application to unstructured-mesh surrogate modeling of nonlinear dynamical systems. This is obtained by replacing Fourier bands with graph-Laplacian frequency bands, and we provide scalable Chebyshev and multilevel approximations for improving long-horizon rollout fidelity. In its full-spectrum form, our approach uses graph Laplacian eigenspaces to provide a graph analogue of Fourier band-power matching, but incurs the high cost of spectral decomposition. As a scalable approximation, we replace exact band projectors with sparse Chebyshev polynomial graph filters, avoiding explicit eigendecomposition. When utilizing multilevel graph architectures, we introduce Graph Laplacian Energy Alignment for Meshes (GLEAM), which applies retained-subspace scale-aware supervision across graph hierarchies so that coarse and fine representations are regularized during autoregressive rollout. Our results show that the proposed spectral losses improve long-horizon rollout fidelity and preserve statistical invariants for the forecasting of turbulent flows on unstructured meshes, compared to deterministic baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Total Variation Distance Estimation in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19510)
*Eric Price,Kevin Tian,Zhiyang Xun,Yusong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大语言模型部署中，由于推理优化（如批处理、自定义内核、量化）导致"相同模型"在不同推理引擎下产生不同分布的问题。作者提出在三种访问模型（样本访问、logits访问、带噪logits访问）下估计两个自回归分布之间总变差距离的算法，复杂度分别为$\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$、$O(n/\varepsilon^2)$和$\widetilde{O}((n+n^2σ^2)/\varepsilon^2)$，并在SGLang与vLLM之间进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM部署中，即使使用相同的固定权重，不同的推理引擎（如SGLang和vLLM）由于采用不同的实现选择和推理优化（批处理、自定义内核、量化等），会产生显著不同的输出分布。现有方法估计两个自回归分布之间的总变差距离存在查询复杂度过高的问题，且缺乏针对不同访问模型（样本、logits、带噪logits）的系统性研究。

Method: 本文针对三种访问模型分别设计算法：(1) 样本访问下，利用自回归结构将TV距离分解为逐位置条件概率差异，通过重要性采样等技术实现$\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$查询复杂度；(2) logits访问下，直接利用logits计算精确条件概率，达到最优的$O(n/\varepsilon^2)$复杂度；(3) 带噪logits访问下，考虑相对误差σ，通过平滑插值实现$\widetilde{O}((n+n^2σ^2)/\varepsilon^2)$复杂度。算法在SGLang和vLLM之间进行实证评估。

Result: 理论上，样本访问下查询复杂度从Meel等人(2025)的$\widetilde{O}(n^3 m/\varepsilon^5)$提升至$\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$；logits访问下达到$O(n/\varepsilon^2)$的最优复杂度；带噪logits访问下实现了两种极端情况之间的平滑插值。实验上，成功测量了SGLang与vLLM之间服务相同权重时的TV距离，并展示了该方法在KL散度无穷大时仍可估计TV距离的鲁棒性和实用性。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM推理引擎之间的分布差异提供了可计算、高效的TV距离估计框架，覆盖了从纯样本到完全logits访问的多种实际场景。理论结果表明存在高效的估计算法，实验验证了方法的实际可行性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Modern LLM deployments use a number of implementation choices and inference optimizations (e.g., batching, custom kernels, and quantization) on top of fixed weights, so two engines serving "the same model" can produce meaningfully different distributions. We study the problem of estimating the total variation (TV) distance between two length-$n$ autoregressive distributions to additive error $\varepsilon$, under three access models. (1) Under sample access, we use $\widetilde{O}(n^2 K/\varepsilon^2)$ queries, where $K$ is the maximum support of the next-token distribution. This improves upon the $\widetilde{O}(n^3 m/\varepsilon^5)$-query estimator of Meel et al. (2025), where $m \geq K$ is the total size of the token alphabet. (2) Under logit access, we use $O(n/\varepsilon^2)$ queries, and this is tight. (3) Under noisy logit access, we smoothly interpolate between the above two guarantees: if probability values are given to relative error $σ$, we use $\widetilde{O}((n+n^2σ^2)/\varepsilon^2)$ queries. We complement our theoretical results with an empirical evaluation of our algorithms, for example measuring the distance between SGLang and vLLM serving identical weights. Our experiments highlight the robustness and practicality of estimating the total variation distance, which remains estimable where the KL divergence is infinite. Our code is available at https://github.com/XunZhiyang/llm-tv-estimation.

</details>


### [134] [Neural Operator Surrogates for Two-Dimensional Neutron Flux Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19388)
*Japan K. Patel,Barry D. Ganapol,Anthony Magliari,Matthew C. Schmidt,Todd A. Wareing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将一维单扫描神经算子研究扩展到二维，使用FNO和UNO三种代理模型映射材料/源场到标量通量，并考察单扫描近似输入和对数通量训练对精度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将一维单扫描神经算子方法扩展到二维问题，探索更高效的粒子输运替代模型，特别是评估单扫描近似作为输入能否提升精度，以及对数通量训练在强衰减区域（如屏蔽问题）的改善效果。

Method: 考虑三种代理模型：FNO直接映射、UNO直接映射，以及以单扫描近似（一次源迭代后的标量通量）为额外输入的FNO。使用离散纵标求解器生成高保真数据，以平均相对L2误差范数评估映射质量，每种模型训练三个随机种子以评估变异性。

Result: 平均相对L2误差范数用于评估映射质量，三种代理模型在不同随机种子下的差异被量化比较。

Conclusion: 单扫描输入是否提升直接映射的精度，以及对数通量训练是否改善强衰减区域的精度——这两个核心问题有待实验验证。

Abstract: This work extends our one-dimensional single-sweep neural-operator studies to two dimensions. We consider one-group transport with isotropic scattering. As in the one-dimensional work, we use Fourier neural operators (FNOs) to approximate the high-fidelity scalar flux. Additionally, we also investigate U-shaped neural operators (UNOs) in this study. We consider three surrogates. The first two map the material and source fields directly to the flux, one using an FNO and one using a UNO. The third is an FNO that additionally takes the scalar flux after one source iteration, the single-sweep approximation, as an input. Each case is solved to high fidelity with a verified discrete-ordinates solver, and an average relative L_2 error norm is used to characterize the quality of the inferred maps. We train every surrogate over three random seeds so that differences between them can be assessed against run-to-run variability. Two questions guide the study: whether the single-sweep input improves accuracy over the direct maps, and whether training on the logarithm of the flux improves accuracy in the strongly attenuated regions relevant to shielding.

</details>


### [135] [Efficient Clustering with Provable Guardrails for LLM Inference at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19704)
*Longshaokan Wang,Wai Tsang Keung,Punit Ghodasara,Roman Wang,Ali Dashti,Francesc Moreno-Noguer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段聚类算法（Mini-batch K-Means + 贪心代表选择，等价于集合覆盖），在数千万样本规模下保证每个样本与其簇代表之间的相似度下限和属性精确匹配，推理成本降低50倍，已部署于3800万用户的个性化推荐系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM应用的推理成本和延迟成为瓶颈，现有聚类方法无法同时保证每个样本的质量控制（簇内相似度下限、类别属性精确匹配）和可扩展性。

Method: 两阶段算法：第一阶段用Mini-batch K-Means生成初始簇；第二阶段在初始簇内贪心选择代表（等价于嵌入空间中α球的Johnson-Chvatal集合覆盖启发式），硬性保证相似度和属性约束。时间O(nd + n²d/K)，内存O(nd + n²/K²)。

Result: 在内部和公开数据集上比常见聚类方法快10-1000倍，可扩展到其他方法无法处理的数据规模。在3800万用户的个性化推荐系统中，推理成本和延迟降低50倍，同时保持个性化效果并实现生产上线。

Conclusion: 该算法首次在数千万样本规模下同时满足每样本质量控制、属性精确匹配和高可扩展性，工程实践验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Scaling LLM-based applications to millions of users is bottlenecked by the inference cost and latency of modern foundation models. A natural fix is to cluster the inputs and call the LLM only on cluster representatives, letting other members inherit the output -- but this is only safe if each member is measurably close to its representative. Existing clustering methods do not offer such per-sample quality control at scale: none jointly guarantee a minimal within-cluster similarity, exact matching of categorical attributes, and scalability to tens of millions of samples. We propose a two-stage algorithm that generates initial clusters with Mini-batch K-Means, then greedily selects representatives within each initial cluster -- a step equivalent to the Johnson-Chvatal heuristic for Set Cover over alpha-balls in embedding space. The algorithm enforces the similarity and attribute guardrails exactly by construction, and runs in $O(nd + n^2 d/K)$ time and $O(nd + n^2/K^2)$ memory for $n$ samples, feature dimension $d$, and $K$ initial clusters -- linear in $n$ when $K$ grows proportionally with $n$. We provide benchmarks against common clustering methods on internal and public datasets: our method not only delivers per-sample guardrails but also runs 10-1000x faster and scales to data sizes where most standard methods become intractable. Deployed on 38 million customers for a persona-based recommender, the clustering method cut downstream cost and latency by 50-fold while preserving personalization and unblocked the production launch.

</details>


### [136] [The Orthogonalized Read Is a Removable Training Scaffold for Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19390)
*Keston Aquino-Michaels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 正交化mLSTM记忆矩阵的读取过程（5次Newton-Schulz迭代）能显著提升噪声联想召回，但这种提升并非真正的记忆改善，而是通过重新调节学习问题来帮助模型逃离训练平台期。该效应具有自一致性、均匀性和可移除性，且大部分已发表增益实际上与架构无关，而是源于逃逸危险率的热/噪声规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示正交化mLSTM记忆矩阵读取操作带来的性能提升背后的真正机制，澄清该提升并非记忆能力的改善，而是对学习动态的重调节。

Method: 通过四组对照实验验证：1) 使用精确递归最小二乘读取（Mesa层）复现效果；2) 在超参数网格上测量逃逸危险率；3) 对失败模型仅推理阶段施加正交化；4) 在逃逸触发后退火移除正交化。同时通过直接解码记忆状态分析失败模型的存储情况。

Result: 正交化读取将逃逸危险率提高约6倍且与任务难度无关，扩大了有效学习率区间；失败模型的记忆状态中约有一半关联以线性可恢复形式存在；训练平台期本质上是读取失败而非存储失败。

Conclusion: 联想召回基准测试在用于架构选择时部分衡量的是可训练性而非记忆能力；该系统作为完全可仪器化的"涌现"模型生物体，证明了尖锐的行为阈值源自对逐渐累积结构的审查度量。

Abstract: A recent report finds that orthogonalizing the mLSTM memory matrix at read time (five Newton-Schulz iterations, trained through) substantially improves noisy associative recall. The effect replicates, but it is not a memory improvement. Training on this task is a long chance plateau followed by a sharp escape, and the orthogonalized read acts by re-conditioning the learning problem during the plateau. Three properties establish this. It must be self-consistent: an exact recursive least-squares read (the Mesa layer) reproduces it, while straight-through halves, delta-rule writes, frozen random keys, and plain normalization all fail. It is uniform: across a learning-rate x hardness grid it multiplies the escape hazard roughly six-fold with no detectable hardness dependence, widening the workable learning-rate corridor that narrows for the baseline. And it is removable: applied to failed models at inference it rescues none, and annealed away on an escape-triggered schedule it leaves numerically stock mLSTMs at full accuracy. Much of the published gain needs no architecture at all -- solved-rate at a fixed budget measures escape hazard, which follows a heat/noise law (learning-rate elasticity +3.0, gradient-noise elasticity -1.65) under which the original vocab-96 result is a large-batch noise condition rather than a capacity one. Decoding the memory state directly shows failed models carry roughly half their associations in linearly recoverable form: the plateau is a readout failure over half-written storage. Two conclusions travel beyond the intervention: recall benchmarks used for architecture selection partly measure trainability, and the system is a fully instrumented model organism of "emergence," in which a sharp behavioral threshold demonstrably arises from a censored metric over gradually accumulating structure.

</details>


### [137] [Asymptotically Optimal Regret for Reinforcement Learning without Horizon Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19854)
*Runlong Zhou,Zihan Zhang,Maryam Fazel,Simon S. Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域时间齐次表格型MDP，提出了一种无Horizon依赖的新算法，实现了$\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK}+S^8A^3)$的遗憾上界，彻底消除了$\log H$项，达到了渐近最优的Horizon-free遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗憾上界存在$\log H$依赖或$S$的多项式阶数过高的问题，例如Zhang et al. (2021)的$\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK\log H}+S^2A\log H)$仍含有$\log H$项，Zhang et al. (2022)的$\tilde O(\sqrt{S^9A^3K})$渐近表现不佳。本文旨在完全消除$H$的依赖并改进$S$的依赖，达到渐近最优。

Method: (1) 利用最优值函数$V_h^*$关于$h$的单调性，避免直接对$H$个值函数进行联合界导致的$\min\{\log H,S\}$因子；(2) 将值函数非平凡地投影到$S$维网格上；(3) 提出Horizon截断论证，实现基于奖励的探索，消除单独的奖励无关探索阶段；(4) 设计切割奖励（cutting bonus），同时保持乐观性和规划所需的单调性；(5) 证明时间齐次MDP的新的总偏差界，控制切割奖励中的剪裁方差项，仅关于$S$多项式依赖而无$H$依赖。

Result: 遗憾上界为$\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK}+S^8A^3)$，其中$\tilde O$隐藏$\mathsf{poly}\log(S,A,K,1/\delta)$因子。该界与$H$无关，且匹配上下文赌博机的下界$\Omega(\sqrt{SAK})$（对数因子内），实现了渐近最优的horizon-free遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 本文完全消除了先前工作中$\log H$的依赖，并大幅改进了$S$的多项式阶数（从$S^{9/2}$降至$S^8$），证明了时间齐次表格型MDP中Horizon-free遗憾渐近最优性是可达的。三个关键技术（Horizon截断、切割奖励、新总偏差界）为后续Horizon-free强化学习研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We study horizon-free regret minimization for finite-horizon time-homogeneous tabular Markov decision processes with $S$ states, $A$ actions, horizon $H$, and per-trajectory total reward bounded by $1$.
  We propose a new algorithm and prove a regret upper bound \[\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK}+S^8A^3)\] with failure probability $δ$, where $K$ is the number of episodes and $\tilde O(\cdot)$ hides $\mathsf{poly}\log(S,A,K,1/δ)$.
  Thus, the regret is $H$-free and asymptotically optimal, matching the contextual-bandit lower bound $Ω(\sqrt{SAK})$ up to logarithmic factors. This completely removes the $\log H$ dependence from the previous $\tilde O(\sqrt{SAK\log H}+S^2A\log H)$ guarantee of Zhang et al. (2021), and drastically improves the prior best horizon-free regret $\tilde O(\sqrt{S^9A^3K})$ of Zhang et al. (2022) asymptotically.
  The main technical difficulty is that the optimal value functions $\{V_h^*\}_{h=1}^H$ are time-inhomogeneous even though the transition kernel is time-homogeneous. A direct union bound over all value functions typically incurs an additional $\min\{\log H,S\}$ factor. We avoid this factor by (i) exploiting the monotonicity of $V_h^*$ in $h$ and (ii) non-trivially projecting the value functions onto an $S$-dimensional grid.
  Our analysis relies on three additional ingredients. First, we introduce a horizon-truncation argument that enables reward-based exploration and removes the cost of a separate reward-free exploration phase. Second, we design a cutting bonus that preserves both optimism and the monotonicity needed for planning. Third, we prove a new bound on total deviation for time-homogeneous MDPs, which controls the clipped variance terms in the cutting bonus with adjustable polynomial dependence on $S$ and without any dependence on $H$. Together, these tools yield an asymptotically optimal horizon-free regret guarantee.

</details>


### [138] [LAARA: Layer-Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19391)
*Ashutosh Tripathi,Surya Deep Singh,Pranab Sahoo,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAARA提出了一种基于Fisher信息引导的自适应秩分配框架，无需搜索即可动态为不同Transformer层分配不同的适配器秩，在GLUE和MathInstruct基准上以更少参数匹配或超越LoRA、AdaLoRA等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩适配方法（如LoRA）为所有Transformer层分配相同的适配器秩，忽略了各层对适应需求的异质性，导致分配方案本质上是次优的。

Method: 利用训练过程中轻量级对角Fisher估计计算各层的适配重要性，结合投影归一化、对数压缩、混合适配器重要性估计以及投票变更阻尼机制，实现无需搜索的稳定动态秩分配。

Result: 在GLUE和MathInstruct基准上，LAARA以显著更少的可训练参数达到或超越了LoRA、AdaLoRA、DyLoRA和Bitfit等主流方法的性能。

Conclusion: Fisher引导的秩分配为自适应参数高效微调提供了一种有原则且有效的解决方案，证明了基于重要性的异构秩分配策略的优越性。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation is widely used for parameter-efficient fine-tuning, yet existing methods typically assign the same adapter rank to every transformer layer despite their heterogeneous adaptation requirements. In this work, we show theoretically and empirically that uniform rank allocation is fundamentally suboptimal. Motivated by this observation, we propose LAARA (Layer Aware Adaptive Rank Allocation framework), a search-free framework that dynamically allocates ranks using lightweight diagonal Fisher estimates computed during training. LAARA combines projection-wise normalization, logarithmic compression, blended adapter importance estimation, and a vote-to-change dampening mechanism to produce stable and efficient rank adaptation. Experiments on GLUE and MathInstruct benchmark demonstrate that LAARA consistently matches or outperforms popular state of the art approaches such as LoRA, AdaLoRA, DyLoRA, and Bitfit while using significantly fewer trainable parameters. Our results show that Fisher-guided rank allocation provides a principled and effective foundation for adaptive parameter-efficient fine-tuning. The code is publicly available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LAARA-D305/LAARA.py

</details>


### [139] [Active Inference as a Convex Markov Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20152)
*Nikola Milosevic,Nicolás Hinrichs,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明主动推理（AIF）中的期望自由能（EFE）最小化可形式化为凸马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），其中实用项等效于潜在MDP中的奖励最大化，而认知项引入非线性成分。作者推导了镜像下降（MD）算法，将目标围绕当前状态边际局部线性化，产生与演员-评论家方法和动态规划兼容的策略依赖奖励，并指出将世界模型学习与策略优化耦合使主动推理具有执行式强化学习的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将主动推理（AIF）的期望自由能（EFE）最小化框架纳入现代强化学习和优化理论，建立统一的数学形式化，使其能够利用收敛分析和策略改进保证等理论工具。

Method: 将EFE最小化形式化为凸马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）——实用项线性地等价于潜在MDP中的奖励最大化；提出镜像下降（MD）算法，通过对当前状态边际进行局部线性化，得到策略依赖的认知奖励；将世界模型学习与策略优化的耦合解释为执行式强化学习。

Result: EFE最小化可分解为线性实用项和非线性认知项；MD算法产生与演员-评论家方法和动态规划兼容的求解方案；主动推理在结构上等价于执行式强化学习。

Conclusion: 该形式化为主动推理提供了现代强化学习和优化理论的根基，包括收敛分析和策略改进保证，弥合了主动推理与标准强化学习之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: Active Inference (AIF) frames adaptive behavior as the minimization of expected free energy (EFE), combining epistemic and pragmatic objectives within a single variational principle. We frame AIF as policy optimization and show that, for closed-loop control policies, EFE minimization can be formulated as a convex Markov decision process (MDP). In this formulation, the pragmatic terms are linear in the predictive state marginals and therefore equivalent to reward maximization in a latent MDP, while the epistemic value introduces a nonlinear component that distinguishes EFE minimization from standard reinforcement learning. This perspective further reveals the epistemic drive of active inference as a policy-dependent (performative) reward. We analyze finite-horizon, discounted, and average-reward formulations of EFE and derive a mirror descent (MD) algorithm that locally linearizes the objective around the current state marginals, yielding a policy-dependent reward that is compatible with actor-critic methods and dynamic programming. Finally, we argue that coupling world-model learning with policy optimization gives active inference the structure of performative reinforcement learning, providing a route toward grounding active inference within modern reinforcement learning and optimization theory, including convergence analysis and principled policy improvement guarantees.

</details>


### [140] [Predicting Groundwater Arsenic Concentrations Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19392)
*William Xing,Stephanie Yang,Aarush Bandemegal,Anushree Misra,Ananya Kalapatapu,Brennan Lagasse,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含74,000多个砷样本的空间集成数据集，利用k-NN和GIS技术整合全美地下水砷浓度数据，并评估了多种机器学习模型（包括树集成方法、多层感知机和图神经网络）。研究发现，虽然梯度提升树在表格数据中仍属最优，但图神经网络能进一步利用空间依赖性，达到或超越梯度提升树的性能，为地下水砷污染风险制图提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
