Conclusion: PSDA有效解决了UDA中分布匹配损失的高方差问题，通过定制化的配对采样策略实现了方差缩减，从而提升域适应性能。

Abstract: Correlation alignment and the maximum mean discrepancy are two widely used distribution-matching frameworks for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). However, high variance in these losses has been shown to undermine their effectiveness in minibatch optimisation settings. Furthermore, the losses lack finite-sum structure, which renders them incompatible with classical stochastic variance reduction (SVR) methods. This paper proposes Paired Sampling for Domain Adaptation (PSDA), a novel SVR technique tailored to such objectives. PSDA pairs observations both within and across domains, to form quadruplets that are always sampled together during training. The pairings are designed to minimise expected gradient variance, and reduce to solving a set of linear assignment problems. Our simulations demonstrate reduced variance compared to related methods, and experiments on three domain shift datasets show improved target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [212] [Online Variance Reduction for Domain Adaptation on Streaming Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20374)
*Andrea Napoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARROW，首个针对MMD和CORAL损失的在线随机方差缩减算法，通过自适应重加权在线数据流中的小批次，实现与离线算法竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MMD和CORAL损失的离线SVR算法不兼容在线、分布式或增量学习场景，亟需一种能处理流式数据的在线方差缩减方法。

Method: 维护对齐统计量的移动平均参考，自适应重加权到达的小批次以对齐小批次与参考统计量，并采用松弛重加权方案使权重优化问题可解。

Result: 在运行时间、方差缩减程度和目标域准确率方面与离线算法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: ARROW作为首个在线SVR算法，成功将MMD和CORAL的方差缩减扩展至流式数据场景，兼顾效率与性能。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of stochastic variance reduction (SVR) for the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) and correlation alignment (CORAL) loss functions. Although various offline SVR algorithms for these losses have been proposed, these are incompatible with online, distributed, or incremental learning settings. This paper presents Adaptive vaRiance Reduction via Online reWeighting (ARROW), the first online SVR algorithm for the MMD and CORAL for streamed data. The method maintains moving average references of the alignment statistics, and adaptively reweights incoming minibatches so that the minibatch and reference statistics are aligned. Further, we propose a relaxed reweighting scheme so that the ensuing weight-optimisation problem is tractable. In experiments and simulations, we show that ARROW performs competitively with offline algorithms in terms of runtime, degree of variance reduction achieved, and target domain accuracy.

</details>


### [213] [PG-KINN: A Physics-Informed Petrov-Galerkin Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Solving Forward and Inverse PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20378)
*Amirhossein Sadr,Nima Soltani,Vahideh Moghtadaiee,Aida Pakniyat,Dara Rahmati,Saeid Gorgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PG-KINN提出了一种基于Petrov-Galerkin格式的物理信息KAN框架，将KAN试空间与紧支撑分段多项式检验空间结合，通过分部积分降低微分阶数，在裂纹奇异性、应力集中、超弹性、逆参数识别等基准测试中一致优于MLP基线和现有KAN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息学习方法（如基于MLP的PINN）受限于谱偏差和密集参数化，而KAN虽缓解了这些问题但面临损失函数形式选择困境：强形式要求高阶导数和权重调整，能量形式仅适用于自伴算子且本文发现其对于参数识别问题会坍缩到平凡解，边界积分形式需已知基本解。

Method: 提出PG-KINN，采用Petrov-Galerkin格式：试空间为KAN，检验空间为独立且紧支撑的分段多项式空间，通过Gauss-Legendre数值积分进行评估。分部积分降低微分阶数，局部化检验函数将全局残差转化为一组条件良好的单元弱残差。

Result: 在裂纹奇异性、应力集中、Neo-Hookean超弹性、非均匀介质逆参数识别以及复杂几何等多个基准测试中，PG-KINN一致优于传统MLP基线和现有最先进的KAN强形式/能量形式/逆形式方法（PIKAN）。

Conclusion: Petrov-Galerkin耦合KAN试空间与多项式检验空间是AI驱动计算力学中一条鲁棒且精确的路线。

Abstract: Physics-informed learning of partial differential equations (PDEs) has been dominated by multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), whose spectral bias and dense parameterization limit both accuracy and interpretability. Kolmogorov Arnold Networks (KANs) mitigate these limitations because their learnable spline activations are structurally aligned with the piecewise-polynomial bases of classical discretizations. However, the way a PDE is cast into a loss functional is as decisive as the choice of approximator: strong-form residual minimization requires high-order derivatives and heavily weighted losses, the energy (Bubnov-Galerkin) form is restricted to self-adjoint operators and, as we show, collapses to a trivial solution for parameter-identification problems, and boundary integral forms require a known fundamental solution. We propose PG-KINN, a physics-informed KAN built on a Petrov-Galerkin formulation in which the trial space is a KAN and the test space is an independent, compactly supported, piecewise-polynomial space evaluated with Gauss-Legendre quadrature. Integration by parts lowers the differentiation order while retaining applicability to general non-self-adjoint, nonlinear, and inverse problems; the localized test functions turn the global residual into a set of element-wise weak residuals with favorable conditioning. On a suite of benchmarks spanning crack singularities, stress concentration, Neo-Hookean hyperelasticity, inverse parameter identification in heterogeneous media, and complex geometries, PG-KINN consistently outperforms legacy MLP baselines and state-of-the-art KAN-based strong/energy/inverse formulations (PIKAN). These results position the Petrov-Galerkin coupling of KAN trial spaces and polynomial test spaces as a robust and accurate route for AI-based computational mechanics.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [A Bayesian Framework for Built-in Input Dimension Reduction for Gaussian Process Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19498)
*Eric Herrison Gyamfi,Emily L. Kang,Bledar A. Konomi,Guang Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个贝叶斯框架，将高斯过程建模与降维无缝集成，通过在Stiefel流形上施加正交先验并使用哈密顿蒙特卡洛进行后验推断，同时扩展至深度高斯过程，在提高预测性能和不确定性量化方面优于传统两步法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维输入下高斯过程建模面临维度灾难，现有方法通常采用降维与GP拟合分离的两阶段方式，未能充分利用两者之间的协同效应，亟需一种能同时进行降维和GP建模的集成方法。

Method: 构建层次贝叶斯模型，在Stiefel流形上对投影矩阵施加正交先验，利用含测地流运动的哈密顿蒙特卡洛进行后验推断；进一步将框架扩展至深度高斯过程，实现内置降维。

Result: 虽然所提贝叶斯方法计算成本较高，但在预测性能和不确定性量化方面显著优于现有方法，提供了更稳健的替代方案。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯框架为高维GP建模提供了一种原则性且鲁棒的集成降维方法，尽管计算开销增加，但换取了更好的预测精度和不确定性估计。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) modeling is widely used in computational science and engineering. However, fitting a GP to high-dimensional inputs remains challenging due to the curse of dimensionality. While various methods have been proposed to reduce input dimensionality, they typically follow a two-stage approach, performing dimension reduction and GP fitting separately. We introduce a Bayesian framework that seamlessly integrates dimensionality reduction with GP modeling and inference. Our approach, built on a hierarchical Bayesian model with priors on the Stiefel manifold, enforces orthonormality on the projection matrix and enables posterior inference via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo with geodesic flow. Additionally, we extend this framework by incorporating Deep Gaussian Processes (DGP) with built-in dimension reduction, providing a more flexible and powerful tool for complex datasets. Through extensive numerical studies, we demonstrate that while the proposed Bayesian method incurs higher computational costs, it improves predictive performance and uncertainty quantification, providing a principled and robust alternative to existing methods.

</details>


### [215] [Boltzmann-Expected Molecular Design with Decoupled Annealing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19519)
*Selma Moqvist,Richard Beckmann,Ross Irwin,Rocío Mercado,Simon Olsson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DECAF（解耦退火流）框架，将3D分子设计重新定义为玻尔兹曼期望设计。通过分解联合分布为图条件流（作为玻尔兹曼模拟器）和坐标条件流，DECAF利用模拟退火规则在全局平均性质上优化分子图，而非单构象性质。在GEOM-Drugs上验证了其对系综感知优化的有效性，并拓展到多目标优化和高阶矩设计（控制构象分布的方差和偏度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D性质引导生成模型将每个性质绑定到单一结构，忽略了分子真实的玻尔兹曼分布系综。许多关键性质（如自由能、形状描述符）本质上是系综期望，而非单构象性质，因此需要一种能够感知系综的分子设计方法。

Method: DECAF将图与坐标的联合分布分解为两个条件流模型：图条件流p(x|G)作为玻尔兹曼模拟器生成构象系综，坐标条件流p(G|x)从3D信息提出新图。通过交替执行两个流，采用模拟退火接受规则，评分函数基于从p(x|G)采样的系综进行评价。

Result: 在GEOM-Drugs数据集上，系综感知优化使分子的回转半径和溶剂可及表面积持续向目标偏移，而单构象优化在较大的药物样分子上效果退化。DECAF支持多目标权衡，并能进行高阶矩设计，通过MD模拟验证了所设计分子的构象分布可控性。

Conclusion: DECAF证明了系综感知的分子设计框架在药物相关分子优化中的有效性，能够处理更大、更灵活的分子，并且无需重新训练即可切换目标。高阶矩设计为控制分子柔性构象行为提供了新范式。

Abstract: Most 3D properties relevant to molecular design, including free energies and shape descriptors, are $\textit{expectations}$ over the Boltzmann distribution over 3D configurations of a molecular graph. However, existing property-guided generative models tie each property to a single structure, ignoring the underlying ensemble. We recast 3D molecular design as $\textbf{Boltzmann-expected design}$ and realise it with $\textbf{DECAF}$ (Decoupled Annealing Flows), which factorise the joint distribution over graphs and coordinates into two conditional flow models: a graph-conditioned flow $p(x\mid\mathcal{G})$, acting as a $\textit{Boltzmann emulator}$, and a coordinate-conditioned flow $p(\mathcal{G}\mid x)$, proposing new graphs from 3D information. By alternating the two flows, DECAF optimises molecular graphs with a simulated-annealing acceptance rule whose scoring function is evaluated on ensembles drawn from $p(x\mid\mathcal{G})$, making ensemble statistics, not single-conformer properties, the design target. The resulting loop requires no retraining to change objectives. On GEOM-Drugs, we show that ensemble-aware optimisation produces graphs whose mean radius of gyration and solvent-accessible surface area consistently shift toward targets, while single-conformer optimisation degrades on larger drug-like molecules where Boltzmann distributions are broadest. DECAF extends to multi-objective trade-offs and, uniquely among 3D generative models, to $\textbf{higher-moment design}$: jointly optimising an ensemble property's variance and skewness to produce flexible molecules biased to a prescribed conformational regime: we verify the conformational distributions of these higher-moment designs with all-atom MD simulations.

</details>


### [216] [RELTA-SGLD: Relative-Growth Localized Taming for Nonconvex Stochastic-Gradient Langevin Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19544)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出RELTA-SGLD驯化方案，通过相对增长原则和阈值机制稳定超线性随机梯度更新，在非凸SGLD中实现多项式矩稳定性和一阶驻点精度，在Fashion-MNIST上优于未驯化SGLD和TUSLA，接近AdamW性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超线性随机梯度更新在非凸优化中存在不稳定性问题，现有驯化方案过度抑制原始学习漂移，导致收敛性能下降。需要一种既能稳定更新又不过度干扰原始动力学的驯化策略。

Method: 提出RELTA（相对增长驯化）方案：1）阈值机制决定驯化何时启动；2）基于一步Lyapunov稳定性条件推导相对增长原则，确定所需驯化强度；3）采用更轻的λ尺度分母，保留非零远尾返回。

Result: 在非凸SGLD中证明多项式矩稳定性和W1、W2一阶驻点精度，提升半阶和四分之一阶边界。在Fashion-MNIST主动稳定压力下，平均学习指标优于SGLD和TUSLA，与调优AdamW竞争力相当；普通训练中保持几乎未驯化的学习动态。

Conclusion: RELTA-SGLD有效平衡了稳定性和学习动态保留，理论保证更强，实证表现优异，特别是在需要主动稳定的场景下优势明显。

Abstract: We introduce RELTA-SGLD, a taming scheme that stabilizes superlinear stochastic-gradient updates while reducing unnecessary suppression of the original learning drift. A threshold determines where the taming turns on, while a relative-growth principle derived from the one-step Lyapunov stability condition determines the required taming strength. Together, they produce a lighter $λ$-scale denominator and preserve a nonvanishing far-tail return. As a consequence, we prove polynomial moment stability and first-order stationary accuracy in both $W_1$ and $W_2$ for nonconvex SGLD with superlinearly growing stochastic-gradient oracles, improving the corresponding half-order and quarter-order bounds for comparable stochastic-gradient tamed schemes. On Fashion-MNIST under active stabilization pressure, RELTA improves the mean learning metrics over both untamed SGLD and TUSLA and remains competitive with a tuned AdamW reference. In an ordinary-training regime, its lighter localized denominator reduces unnecessary perturbation of the original update and maintains nearly untamed learning dynamics.

</details>


### [217] [Optimal Recalibration of an Online Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19689)
*Lunjia Hu,Kevin Tian,Chutong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在线预测器的重新校准问题，提出了一个基于非平衡Blackwell可接近性框架的在线算法，实现了对Lipschitz真损失的(ε, ε²)重新校准，并证明了该权衡的最优性。作为主要应用，该方法与在线精炼方法结合，同步实现了ε-校准和ε²-校准击败，改进了先前工作的ε依赖关系。实验验证了算法在分布漂移分类数据集上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定任意"提示"预测序列，学习器需要输出经过校准的新预测，同时相对于原始预测保持较小的超额误差（在真损失下）。现有方法未能同时实现最优的校准与校准击败率，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 提出非平衡扩展的同步Blackwell可接近性约简框架，设计在线重新校准算法；2) 对平方损失给出匹配的下界证明；3) 结合在线精炼方法[FH23]实现同步校准与校准击败；4) 推导多提示序列的扩展。

Result: 在T≈ε⁻³轮内达到(ε, ε²)重新校准；证明了平方损失下该权衡的最优性；K₂变体达到近乎最优的校准与校准击败率；回答了[CHJL26]中的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了同步最优校准与校准击败，改进了以往工作的ε依赖关系，为在线预测校准提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of recalibrating an online predictor [KE17, OKS24]: given an arbitrary "hint" sequence of forecasts, the learner must output new predictions that are calibrated while incurring small excess error relative to the original forecasts, under a proper loss. We give an online algorithm that achieves $(\varepsilon, \varepsilon^2)$-recalibration for Lipschitz proper losses in
  $T \approx \varepsilon^{-3}$ rounds, using an imbalanced extension of the recent simultaneous Blackwell approachability reduction framework of [HTY26]. We show that this tradeoff is optimal by proving a matching lower bound for recalibrating against the squared loss. We also prove a companion $\mathcal{K}_2$-recalibration theorem that obtains the same tradeoffs up to a logarithmic factor.
  As our main application, we show how our recalibration algorithms can be combined with the online refinement method of [FH23] to obtain simultaneous $\varepsilon$-calibration and $\varepsilon^2$-calibeating for smooth proper losses at the same asymptotic rate, improving upon prior works that achieved these properties separately or with a worse $\varepsilon$ dependence. In particular, the $\mathcal{K}_2$ variant answers a question of [CHJL26] on simultaneously achieving near-optimal calibeating and calibration rates. We also derive extensions to settings with multiple hint sequences. Finally, we empirically evaluate our algorithms on a classification dataset undergoing distribution shift.

</details>


### [218] [Data-Poisoning Audits for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19692)
*Kwangho Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对观测性因果分析中存在的追加式数据投毒攻击风险，开发了一种审计框架。该方法通过贪婪扫描算法计算给定追加预算下的最坏情况效应变化，并提出了总影响分数来近似考虑模型重拟合的影响，最终将对抗性数据组合风险转化为移动曲线和关键预算值，支持更可靠的因果报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测性因果分析越来越多地跨站点、供应商和系统汇总数据，这使得数据容易受到"仅追加式攻击"——攻击者通过策略性地选择看似合理的记录来改变报告的治疗效应。现有方法缺乏对这种数据投毒风险的系统性审计工具。

Method: 提出一种数据投毒审计框架：(1) 针对逆概率加权估计，分析师指定可行记录目录、追加预算和嵌套源容量，攻击者选择可行子集最大化预设方向上的效应偏移；(2) 固定预处理和 nuisance 拟合时，提出贪婪扫描算法计算精确的有限样本最坏情况偏移；(3) 为考虑 nuisance 重拟合，推导总影响分数，结合每条记录的直接贡献及其通过倾向得分和结果模型产生的间接影响；(4) 进一步获得完全重拟合估计的保守有限预算界。

Result: 大量仿真验证了精确结果的有效性，并表明总影响分数能改善局部重拟合预测。多站点和公开数据分析证明在小追加预算下存在实质性敏感性。所提出的方法将对抗性数据组合风险转化为可量化的移动曲线和关键预算阈值。

Conclusion: 该框架通过量化数据投毒攻击的潜在影响，支持更可靠的因果推断报告和源头层面的安全防护设计。

Abstract: Observational causal analyses increasingly pool records across sites, vendors, and collection systems, creating vulnerability to append-only attacks in which plausible records are strategically selected to alter a reported treatment effect. We develop a data-poisoning audit for augmented inverse-probability-weighted estimation. The analyst specifies a finite catalog of feasible records, an append budget, and nested source capacities, and the adversary selects a feasible subset to maximize movement in a prespecified direction. With preprocessing and nuisance fits held fixed, we propose a greedy scan that computes the exact finite-sample worst-case movement at every append budget. To account for nuisance refitting, we go on to derive a total-influence score combining each record's direct contribution with its effect through the propensity and outcome models. We further obtain a conservative finite-budget bound for the fully refitted estimate. Extensive simulations validate the exact result and show that total influence improves local refit prediction, while multisite and public-data analyses demonstrate material sensitivity at small append budgets. By translating adversarial data-composition risk into movement curves and critical budgets, the framework supports more reliable causal reporting and the design of source-level safeguards.

</details>


### [219] [Non--negative matrix factorization using the \textit{R} package \textsf{nnmf}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20084)
*Volkan Sevinç,Nikolas Kontemeniotis,Theodoros Perdikis,Michail Tsagris*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个新的R语言NMF包，并基于真实数据与两个主流NMF包进行了系统性能比较，评估指标包括计算效率、收敛性、重构精度、内存使用和分解稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多个R语言NMF实现包，但缺乏基于真实数据条件的系统性评估，研究者难以客观选择适合实际应用的包。

Method: 开发一个新的R语言NMF包，并在统一实验框架下，使用真实数据而非模拟数据，与两个主流R NMF包在计算效率、收敛行为、重构精度、内存利用和分解稳定性方面进行对比评估。

Result: 提供了基于真实数据的系统性能比较结果，涵盖了计算效率、收敛性、重构精度、内存使用和稳定性等多维度指标。

Conclusion: 该研究为研究者选择R语言NMF包提供了客观指导，弥补了现有评估中缺乏真实数据验证的空白。

Abstract: Non--negative matrix factorization (NMF) has become an established dimensionality reduction technique for extracting latent structures from non--negative data and has found widespread applications in fields such as bioinformatics, text mining, image analysis, and recommender systems. As the popularity of NMF has increased, numerous \textit{R} packages implementing different optimization strategies and computational frameworks have been developed. Despite their widespread availability, comprehensive evaluations of these implementations under real--world data conditions remain limited. Consequently, researchers often lack objective guidance when selecting an appropriate package for practical applications. This study introduces a new \textit{R} package for NMF and offers asystematic performance comparison with two widely available \textit{R} packages for NMF analysis. Rather than relying on simulated datasets, the evaluation is conducted using real--world data to better reflect the complexity, heterogeneity, and noise characteristics encountered in practical analytical settings. The packages are assessed using a consistent experimental framework, with emphasis on computational efficiency, convergence behavior, reconstruction accuracy, memory utilization, and the stability of the resulting matrix factorization.

</details>


### [220] [Directional Kernel Mean Difference: A Fast Signed Statistic for Univariate Distribution Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20119)
*Shijie Zhong,Jiangfeng Fu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出方向核均值差（DKMD），一种保留分布偏移方向信息的符号统计量，通过积分核均值嵌入差与奇加权函数实现，具有反对称性、对对称差异的免疫性和随机占优下的方向单调性。开发了O(N log N)的前缀-后缀扫描算法，在百万级样本上实现秒级计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大均值差异（MMD）通过平方RKHS距离丢弃了分布偏移的方向信息，无法区分正向和负向偏移。现有符号统计量缺乏核方法的灵活性和非线性表达能力。

Method: 1) 提出DKMD统计量，对核均值嵌入差与固定奇加权函数积分；2) 推导数据驱动的Riemann估计量保持连续渐近一致性；3) 开发O(N log N)前缀-后缀扫描算法替代二次复杂度计算。

Result: 实验表明DKMD能正确隔离方向性偏移与对称扰动，对重尾离群值保持鲁棒，可在数秒内处理百万级样本。

Conclusion: DKMD弥补了MMD方向信息缺失的不足，为单变量分布比较提供了一种兼具理论保证和计算效率的符号统计量。

Abstract: We introduce the Directional Kernel Mean Difference (DKMD), a signed statistic for univariate distribution comparison that preserves the direction of distributional shifts. Unlike the squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), which discards directional information by squaring the RKHS distance, DKMD integrates the difference of kernel mean embeddings against a fixed odd weighting function. This construction yields three structural properties: antisymmetry, immunity to symmetric distributional differences, and directional monotonicity under stochastic dominance. We derive a data-driven Riemann estimator that ensures asymptotic consistency with the continuous formulation, strictly preserving the theoretical guarantees of the signed statistic in empirical evaluations. To overcome the quadratic computational cost of kernel methods, we develop an $O(N \log N)$ prefix--suffix scanning algorithm that exploits the total order of the real line while requiring only $O(N)$ memory. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that DKMD correctly isolates directional shifts from symmetric perturbations, remains robust to heavy-tailed outliers that can flip the sign of the mean difference, and scales to millions of samples in seconds.

</details>


### [221] [Statistical Inference for Rank Allocation in Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20205)
*Yihang Gao,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出StatLoRA，一种基于统计假设检验的低秩适配（LoRA）秩资源分配方法。该方法将LoRA秩分配形式化为统计检验问题，利用优化器轨迹的中心极限理论推导出组件分数的渐近正态性，并通过p值决定组件的保留或剪枝。在DeBERTaV3-base、BART-Large和Qwen2.5-7B上的实验表明，StatLoRA在自然语言理解、生成和问答任务上达到或优于vanilla LoRA、AdaLoRA和IGU-LoRA的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应秩分配方法主要依赖基于梯度敏感性和不确定性的启发式重要性分数，缺乏明确的统计解释。因此，作者希望将秩分配问题形式化为统计假设检验，为其提供严格的统计理论基础。

Method: StatLoRA将每个LoRA组件关联一个检验统计量，通过估计p值决定组件的保留或剪枝。方法核心是建立了随机优化器轨迹的中心极限理论，证明了包括AdamW在内的广泛优化器的渐近正态性，并推导了组件分数的渐近分布，从而支持假设检验过程的有效性。

Result: 在DeBERTaV3-base、BART-Large和Qwen2.5-7B模型上，针对自然语言理解、自然语言生成和问答任务，StatLoRA在匹配的秩预算下实现了与vanilla LoRA、AdaLoRA和IGU-LoRA相当或更优的性能。敏感性分析和经验诊断验证了假设检验分配规则的稳定性，并为组件分数的渐近理论提供了经验证据。

Conclusion: StatLoRA通过统计假设检验框架为LoRA秩分配提供了严格的统计理论基础，在固定参数预算下能够有效平衡效率与性能，且具有良好的稳定性和可解释性。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for large language models. Since different modules and layers may contribute unequally to downstream adaptation, allocating rank resources under a fixed parameter budget is an important problem for balancing efficiency, expressiveness, and generalization. Existing adaptive rank methods address this problem mainly through carefully designed importance scores constructed from gradient-derived sensitivity and uncertainty measures, without an explicit statistical interpretation. In this paper, we formulate LoRA rank allocation as a statistical hypothesis testing problem and propose StatLoRA, a statistical inference-based rank allocation method. StatLoRA associates each LoRA component with a test statistic and uses estimated p-values to determine which components should be retained or pruned under a prescribed rank budget. The proposed testing procedure is supported by our central limit theory for stochastic optimizer trajectories. In particular, we establish asymptotic normality for a broad class of commonly used optimizers in deep learning, including AdamW, and derive the corresponding asymptotic distributions for the proposed component scores used in hypothesis testing. We evaluate StatLoRA on LoRA fine-tuning of DeBERTaV3-base, BART-Large, and Qwen2.5-7B across natural language understanding, natural language generation, and question answering tasks. Experiments show that StatLoRA achieves comparable or better performance than vanilla LoRA, AdaLoRA, and IGU-LoRA under matched rank budgets. Sensitivity analyses and empirical diagnostics further support the stability of the proposed hypothesis-testing-based allocation rule and provide empirical evidence for the asymptotic theory of component scores.

</details>


### [222] [Adaptive Bayesian Online Learning via Expert Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20239)
*Jungbin Jun,Ilsang Ohn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将贝叶斯更新规则视为专家，通过聚合贝叶斯专家（Bayesian experts）进行在线学习，使得聚合模型能够与事后最优专家竞争。在共形推断和高斯过程回归中的应用表明，该方法能自适应地匹配强专家表现，且无需事先选择专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯在线学习的性能高度依赖于推断选择（如学习率、先验分布、变分族），而这些选择通常需要在看到数据流之前固定，缺乏自适应性。作者希望解决这一局限性，使得在线学习能够自动适应最佳推断策略。

Method: 将不同的贝叶斯更新规则视为专家，根据序列预测损失对专家进行聚合（aggregation），并证明聚合结果能在聚合成本范围内与事后最优专家竞争。将该框架实例化到在线共形推断和高斯过程回归中。

Result: 在共形推断中，得到了自适应共形推断的平滑贝叶斯版本，实现了长期随机化覆盖；在高斯过程回归中，得到了累积预测KL风险的oracle不等式，并实现对未知Hölder光滑性的自适应（达到对数因子级别）。实验表明聚合模型能够跟踪强专家，无需oracle专家选择。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种无需预先固定推断选择即可实现贝叶斯在线学习自适应的方法，在多个应用场景中达到了与最优专家竞争的性能。

Abstract: Bayesian online learning promises uncertainty-aware prediction on data streams, but its performance hinges on inferential choices, including learning rates, prior distributions and variational families, which are usually fixed before seeing the stream. We address this by treating Bayesian update rules as experts and aggregating the Bayesian experts according to sequential predictive losses. We prove that the resulting aggregate competes with the best expert in hindsight at an aggregation cost determined by how each expert's per-round performance is evaluated. We instantiate the framework in online conformal inference and Gaussian process regression. The conformal inference application yields a smoothed Bayesian counterpart of adaptive conformal inference with long-run randomized coverage, while the Gaussian process application gives an oracle inequality in cumulative predictive Kullback-Leibler risk and adaptation to unknown Hölder smoothness up to logarithmic factors. Experiments show that the aggregate tracks strong experts without oracle expert selection.

</details>


### [223] [Adaptive deep nonparametric regression from dependent data under covariate shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20309)
*William Kengne,Ehud Mossa Ockegna*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量偏移下非参数分位数回归和Huber回归问题，提出了一种稀疏惩罚深度神经网络（SPDNN）估计器，并建立了非渐近误差界，该估计器在密度比未知时采用两阶段预训练策略，可自适应达到极小化最优收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中源分布与目标分布往往不同（协变量偏移），导致标准度量失效，需要开发能适应分布差异的稳健估计方法。

Method: 提出稀疏惩罚深度神经网络（SPDNN）估计器，利用密度比校正分布差异；当密度比未知时，采用两阶段预训练：第一步用最小二乘SPDNN估计密度比，第二步用重加权SPDNN估计回归函数。理论分析基于广义Bernstein型不等式，适用于i.i.d.、φ-混合、强混合及C-混合过程。

Result: 建立了分位数回归和Huber回归的非渐近误差界，证明估计器在Hölder光滑函数类上可自适应达到（至多对数因子）极小化最优收敛速率，适用于i.i.d.数据及多种经典时间序列模型。

Conclusion: SPDNN估计器在处理协变量偏移的依赖数据回归问题中具有理论最优性和适应性。

Abstract: Covariate shift often occurs because, in many real applications, the source and the target observations may be generated from different distributions. In this case, the standard metric under the source distribution is not appropriate. This paper considers deep neural network estimators for nonparametric quantile and Huber regression under covariate shift and from dependent observations. We deal with a generalized Bernstein-type inequality that is satisfied by many classical models, including i.i.d. observations, $φ$-mixing, strong mixing, and $\mathcal{C}$-mixing processes. To perform the covariate shift phenomenon, we propose a sparse-penalized deep neural network (SPDNN) estimator that takes into account the discrepancy between the source and target distributions of the data. When the density ratio (between the source and target distributions of the covariate) is unknown, a two steps pre-training procedure is carried out: the first step is devoted to the construction of a least squares SPDNN estimator of the density ratio; which is used in the second step to perform a pre-training reweighted SPDNN estimator of the regression function. For both the quantile and the Huber regression, non-asymptotic error bounds of the proposed SPDNN estimators are established in the class of Hölder smooth functions. These estimators can adaptively attain (up to a logarithmic factor) the minimax optimal convergence rate from i.i.d. data as well as from several classical time series models.

</details>
